####################################################################################################
FILE: README.md
####################################################################################################




> A **LangGraph**-powered, six-node agentic NL-to-SQL engine for banking
> term-deposit compliance analytics.  Ask natural-language questions;
> receive precise SQLite results.

---

## Table of Contents

1. [Quick Start](#quick-start)
2. [Architecture](#architecture)
3. [Configuration](#configuration)
4. [Running the Application](#running-the-application)
5. [Adding a New Database](#adding-a-new-database)
6. [Running the Tests](#running-the-tests)
7. [API Reference](#api-reference)
8. [Documentation](#documentation)

## Quick Start

```bash
git clone <repo-url>
cd COMPLIANCE-CHATBOT-DLH

python -m venv .venv
source .venv/bin/activate      # Linux / macOS
.venv\Scripts\activate         # Windows

pip install -r requirements.txt

cp .env.example .env

python data/seed_synthetic_data.py

python application/Launcher.py
```

Open **http://127.0.0.1:8100** in your browser.

---

## Architecture


```
[START]
   │
   ▼
intent_agent  ──(unclear)──► clarify_node ──► [END]
   │
   │  (clear intent)
   ▼
schema_agent
   │
   ▼
sql_writer_agent  ◄──────────────────────────────────────┐
   │                                                     │ retry
   ▼                                                     │
sql_review_agent ──(passed)──► sql_validator_agent ──────┤
   │                                  │                  │
   │ (max retries / hard error)       │ (valid)          │ (invalid → retry)
   ▼                                  ▼
[END: error]                    executor_agent ──► [END]
```

| Node | Module | LLM? | Role |
|---|---|---|---|
| `intent_agent` | `genai/nl2sql_agent/intent.py` | ✗ | Rule-based intent gate + normalisation |
| `schema_agent` | `genai/nl2sql_agent/schema.py` | ✗ | Live PRAGMA schema + TOML descriptions |
| `sql_writer_agent` | `genai/nl2sql_agent/sql_writer_agent.py` | ✓ | Single LLM call — SQL generation |
| `sql_review_agent` | `genai/nl2sql_agent/sql_review.py` | ✗ | Safety + structural + table existence |
| `sql_validator_agent` | `genai/nl2sql_agent/sql_validator.py` | ✗ | sqlglot AST + dialect check |
| `executor_agent` | `genai/nl2sql_agent/executor.py` | ✗ | Read-only SQLite execution |

---


## Configuration


Copy `.env.example` to `.env` and set your DLH credentials:

```bash
MODEL__PROVIDER=dlh
MODEL__MODEL_NAME=google/gemma-4-31b-it
MODEL__DLH_BASE_URL=http://<your-dlh-host>:9000/api/v1
MODEL__DLH_API_KEY=EMPTY
```


| File | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `genai/config/databases.toml` | ★ DB + table metadata registry |
| `genai/config/query.toml` | Row-limit policy (`enforce_default_limit`, `default_limit`) |
| `genai/config/llm.toml` | LLM provider defaults (overridden by `.env`) |
| `genai/config/application.toml` | App name, host, port |
| `genai/config/logging.toml` | Rotating file handler configuration |


```toml
[query]
enforce_default_limit = false  # set true to auto-append LIMIT when SQL has none
default_limit         = 10     # rows returned when LIMIT is auto-added (if enforce=true)
max_display_rows      = 50     # hard ceiling enforced via fetchmany()
```

---



```bash
python /home/cdsw/src/dlh_ai/application/Launcher.py
```

Starts both processes, auto-finds free ports, waits for the backend to be
ready, and monitors both with a watchdog loop.


```bash
uvicorn application.main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8000 --reload
```


```bash
python -m genai.nl2sql_agent.main "How many KYC N accounts are there?"
```

---


**No Python code changes are needed.** Edit only
`genai/config/databases.toml`:

```toml
[databases.MY_NEW_DB]
db_file     = "data/comp_db/MY_NEW_DB.db"
description = "Description of the new database."

[databases.MY_NEW_DB.tables.MY_TABLE]
description  = "Plain-English summary for the LLM prompt."
filter_query = "SELECT * FROM MY_TABLE WHERE BRANCH = '{branch}'"

[databases.MY_NEW_DB.tables.MY_TABLE.columns]
COL1 = "Human-readable column 1 description"
COL2 = "Human-readable column 2 description"

[databases.MY_NEW_DB.tables.MY_TABLE.few_shots]
examples = """
Question: Show all rows where COL1 is active.
Response:
<begin_sql_response>
SELECT * FROM MY_TABLE WHERE COL1 = 'active';
<end_sql_response>
"""
```

Restart the server (or call `db_registry.reload()`) to pick up the changes.

See [`docs/adding-new-database.md`](docs/adding-new-database.md) for details.

## Running the Tests

```bash
pytest

pytest --cov=genai --cov=application --cov-report=term-missing

pytest tests/test_intent_agent.py -v

pytest -k "executor" -v
```

Test modules:

| File | Coverage |
|---|---|
| `tests/test_intent_agent.py` | IntentAgent — all 4 checks |
| `tests/test_schema_agent.py` | SchemaAgent — context building |
| `tests/test_sql_review_agent.py` | SQLReviewAgent — all validation dimensions |
| `tests/test_sql_validator.py` | SQLValidatorAgent — AST validation |
| `tests/test_executor_agent.py` | ExecutorAgent — SELECT execution + edge cases |
| `tests/test_compliance_processor.py` | ComplianceProcessor — request/response mapping |
| `tests/test_schemas.py` | Pydantic schema validation |
| `tests/test_db_registry.py` | databases.toml registry loading |
| `tests/test_utils.py` | All utility helpers |
| `tests/test_fastapi_app.py` | FastAPI endpoints (ASGI test client) |

## API Reference

### `GET /health`

```json
{ "status": "ok", "version": "2.0.0" }
```


**Request**

```json
{
  "question_id": "req-001",
  "question": "Show accounts where KYC flag is N and age is above 60."
}
```

**Response**

```json
{
  "question_id": "req-001",
  "sql_query": "SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL WHERE KYC_FLG = 'N' AND AGE_YRS > 60 LIMIT 10;",
  "df_output": [
    { "BRCD": "001", "CUST_NBR": "C001", "AGE_YRS": 65, "KYC_FLG": "N" }
  ],
  "result_columns": ["BRCD", "CUST_NBR", "AGE_YRS", "KYC_FLG"],
  "result_summary": "Query returned 1 record(s) (showing first 10 rows). Columns: BRCD, CUST_NBR, AGE_YRS, KYC_FLG.",
  "error": null
}
```


Same request body — returns the full agent stage audit trail in addition to
SQL and result rows.


Reconfigure and test the LLM connection at runtime without restarting the
server.

---


## Design Principles

* **One LLM call per query** — only `SQLWriterAgent` calls the LLM; all
  other nodes are deterministic and fast.
* **TOML-first** — all prompts, database metadata, and configuration live
  in TOML files; no hardcoded strings in Python modules.
* **Zero-code database adds** — add a new database by editing
  `databases.toml` only.
* **Read-only safety** — every SQL execution is wrapped in a
  `BEGIN DEFERRED` transaction that is unconditionally rolled back.
* **Defence in depth** — SQL passes through three independent validation
  layers (regex review → sqlglot AST → SQLite error handling) before
  results are returned.


####################################################################################################
FILE: config.py
####################################################################################################


from pathlib import Path

from pydantic import BaseModel
from pydantic_settings import (
    BaseSettings,
    PydanticBaseSettingsSource,
    SettingsConfigDict,
    TomlConfigSettingsSource,
)


class Common(BaseModel):
    project_id: str
    connection_name: str
    mlflow_experiment_name: str


class Data(BaseModel):
    dymmy: str #FIXME - change it based on actual requirement



class Training(BaseModel):
    dymmy: str #FIXME - change it based on actual requirement


class Inference(BaseModel):
    dymmy: str #FIXME - change it based on actual requirement


# the Settings model
class Settings(BaseSettings):
    """
    Application settings loaded from constructor args, environment variables,
    .env, and TOML.

    Nested fields can be overridden from environment variables by using the
    `env_nested_delimiter="__"` separator.
settings
    Example overrides:
    - COMMON__DUMMY1=value
    - COMMON__EXPERIMENT_NAME=value
    - DATA__SOURCE_TABLE=value
    - DATA__SPARK_EXECUTOR_MEMORY=value

    For example, `DATA__SOURCE_TABLE=prod.table` overrides:
    settings.data.source_table
    """

    common: Common
    data: Data
    training: Training
    inference: Inference

    # Use "__" to target nested fields from environment variables.
    # Example:
    #   COMMON__EXPERIMENT_NAME=my_run
    #   DATA__FEATURE_TABLE=my_feature_table
    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(env_nested_delimiter="__")

    @classmethod
    def settings_customise_sources(
        cls,
        settings_cls: type[BaseSettings],
        init_settings: PydanticBaseSettingsSource,
        env_settings: PydanticBaseSettingsSource,
        dotenv_settings: PydanticBaseSettingsSource,
        file_secret_settings: PydanticBaseSettingsSource,
    ) -> tuple[PydanticBaseSettingsSource, ...]:
        return (
            env_settings,  # 1nd priority - environment variables like DATA__SOURCE_TABLE
            dotenv_settings,  # 2rd priority - .env file entries using the same nested format
            TomlConfigSettingsSource(
                settings_cls
            ),  # 3th priority - config.toml defaults
            init_settings,  # 4th priority - direct values passed to Settings(...)
        )

    def save_as_toml(self) -> None:
        """Save the current settings to a TOML file."""
        import toml

        _file = self.model_config.get("toml_file")
        with open(_file, "w") as f:
            toml.dump(self.model_dump(), f)


def ensure_config_env(config_path: Path | str | None = None) -> Path:
    """Ensure that the given config path is actually indeed present. Return resolved path"""
    if not config_path:
        # the default config path
        p = Path(__file__).parent.parent / "config.toml"
    else:
        p = Path(config_path)

    if p.is_file():
        return p.resolve()
    else:
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Config file not found at: {p}")


def get_settings(config_path: str | Path | None = None) -> Settings:
    """Get the application settings"""
    resolved = ensure_config_env(config_path)

    # set toml file path for Settings before instantiation so BaseSettings reads it
    Settings.model_config["toml_file"] = resolved.as_posix()

    return Settings()

# Root-level conftest.py
# Prevent pytest from attempting to import the root __init__.py, which contains
# relative imports (`from .schemas import ...`) that belong to a legacy package
# context and cannot be resolved when pytest runs from the project root.
collect_ignore = ["__init__.py"]

    "N999",    # Invalid module name — project folder name contains hyphens (not our code)
    "TRY003",  # Long exception messages are fine here

    "TRY401",  # verbose-raise — intentional in some catch blocks
    "S101",    # assert used in tests
    "PLW0603", # global statement — intentional module-level singletons
    "PLR0913", # too-many-arguments — factory functions intentionally verbose
    "PLR0917", # too-many-positional-arguments — same as above

FILE: application\Launcher.py
####################################################################################################


import os
import subprocess
import time
from pathlib import Path


def launch_compliance_bot() -> None:
    """Launch the FastAPI backend and the Gradio/HTTP UI server together."""
    # --- 1. CONFIGURATION ---
    # Resolve project root from this file's location: Launcher.py lives in
    # <project_root>/application/, so the root is one level up.
    PROJECT_ROOT = str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent)
    BACKEND_FILE = Path(PROJECT_ROOT) / "application/main.py"
    UI_FILE = Path(PROJECT_ROOT) / "application/run_ui.py"

    BACKEND_PORT: str = "8001"
    UI_PORT: str = os.environ.get("CDSW_APP_PORT", "8100")

    print("\n Checking environment...")

    if not BACKEND_FILE.exists():
        print(f" X ERROR: Backend file not found at {BACKEND_FILE}")
        return
    if not UI_FILE.exists():
        print(f" X ERROR: UI file not found at {UI_FILE}")
        return

    # Setup Environment Variables
    env: dict[str, str] = os.environ.copy()
    env["CB_BACKEND_URL"] = f"http://127.0.0.1:{BACKEND_PORT}"
    env["CDSW_APP_PORT"] = UI_PORT

    # --- CRITICAL FIX: Add the project root to PYTHONPATH ---
    # This allows uvicorn to find the 'application' module
    existing_pythonpath: str = env.get("PYTHONPATH", "")
    env["PYTHONPATH"] = f"{PROJECT_ROOT}:{existing_pythonpath}" if existing_pythonpath else PROJECT_ROOT

    print("Starting Compliance Bot Suite...")

    backend_proc: subprocess.Popen[bytes] | None = None
    ui_proc: subprocess.Popen[bytes] | None = None

    try:
        # --- 2. START BACKEND ---
        print(f" Launching Backend (FastAPI) on port {BACKEND_PORT}...")
        backend_proc = subprocess.Popen(
            ["python", "-m", "uvicorn", "application.main:app", "--host", "127.0.0.1", "--port", BACKEND_PORT],
            env=env,
        )

        # IMPORTANT: Give the backend time to initialize LangGraph and the API
        print(" Waiting 7 seconds for Backend to stabilize...")
        time.sleep(7)

        # --- 3. START UI SERVER ---
        print(f" Launching UI Server on port {UI_PORT}...")
        ui_proc = subprocess.Popen(
            ["python", str(UI_FILE)],
            env=env,
        )

        print("\n" + "=" * 60)
        print(" SYSTEMS STARTED")
        print(f"Backend Proxy: http://127.0.0.1:{BACKEND_PORT}")
        print(f"Frontend UI:   http://127.0.0.1:{UI_PORT}")
        print("=" * 60)
        print("\n Use the CML-provided link for port 8100 to open the UI.")
        print(" Press Ctrl+C to stop both servers.\n")

        # Keep the main thread alive so the background processes keep running
        while True:
            time.sleep(1)
            # If either process crashes, let the user know
            if backend_proc.poll() is not None:
                print("\n BACKEND CRASHED! Check terminal logs above.")
                break
            if ui_proc.poll() is not None:
                print("\n UI SERVER CRASHED! Check terminal logs above.")
                break

    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\n XXXXX------Shutting down servers-----XXXXX")
    finally:
        if backend_proc is not None:
            backend_proc.terminate()
        if ui_proc is not None:
            ui_proc.terminate()
        print(" All processes stopped.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    launch_compliance_bot()

# Compliance Bot — Application Interfaces

This folder contains three ways to run and interact with the **Compliance Bot**
LangGraph NL-to-SQL backend.

---

## Table of Contents

1. [Project structure](#project-structure)
2. [One-time setup](#one-time-setup)
3. [Interface 1 — Backend only (`main.py`)](#interface-1--backend-only-mainpy)
4. [Interface 2 — Full UI (`run_ui.py`)](#interface-2--full-ui-run_uipy)
5. [Interface 3 — Jupyter Notebook (`notebook.ipynb`)](#interface-3--jupyter-notebook-notebookipynb)
6. [Environment variables reference](#environment-variables-reference)
7. [API reference](#api-reference)
8. [Troubleshooting](#troubleshooting)

---

## Project structure

```
COMPLIANCE-CHATBOT-DLH-V2/          ← project root (always run commands from here)
│
├── application/
│   ├── main.py          ← FastAPI backend (Interface 1)
│   ├── run_ui.py        ← Self-contained HTML/proxy UI server (Interface 2)
│   ├── notebook.ipynb   ← Jupyter notebook client (Interface 3)
│   ├── Launcher.py      ← Optional: launch backend + UI in one process
│   └── README.md        ← this file
│
├── genai/               ← LangGraph agents, core, prompts
├── utils/               ← shared config, logger, registry, helpers
├── data/                ← SQLite database + seed scripts
└── requirements.txt
```

> **All commands below must be run from the project root**, not from inside
> the `application/` folder.

---

## One-time setup

### 1. Create and activate a virtual environment

**Linux / macOS**
```bash
python3 -m venv .venv
source .venv/bin/activate
```

**Windows CMD**
```cmd
python -m venv .venv
.venv\Scripts\activate.bat
```

**Windows PowerShell**
```powershell
python -m venv .venv
.venv\Scripts\Activate.ps1
```

### 2. Install dependencies

```bash
pip install -r requirements.txt
```

### 3. Seed the database (first time only)

```bash
python data/seed_synthetic_data.py --reset --count 2000
```

This creates `data/comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db` with 2 000 synthetic compliance
records.

### 4. Configure the LLM (optional — `.env` file)

Copy the template and fill in your credentials:

```bash
cp .env.example .env   # if a template exists
```

Or create `.env` manually at the project root:

```dotenv
# DLH on-prem inference server
DLH_BASE_URL=http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1
DLH_API_KEY=EMPTY
DLH_VERIFY_SSL=false

# Logging
LOG_LEVEL=INFO
LOG_DIR=data/logs
```

---

## Interface 1 — Backend only (`main.py`)

`main.py` starts a **FastAPI + uvicorn** server that exposes the full REST API.
Use this when you want to query the bot programmatically (curl, Postman,
Python `requests`, or the notebook).

### Start the server

**Linux / macOS**
```bash
python application/main.py
```

**Windows CMD**
```cmd
python application\main.py
```

**Windows PowerShell**
```powershell
python application\main.py
```

The server starts on **`http://127.0.0.1:8000`** by default.

### Alternative — explicit uvicorn invocation

```bash
uvicorn application.main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8000 --reload
```

`--reload` enables hot-reload on code changes (development only).

### Interactive API docs

Once the server is running, open in your browser:

| URL | Description |
|-----|-------------|
| `http://127.0.0.1:8000/docs` | Swagger UI — try endpoints directly |
| `http://127.0.0.1:8000/redoc` | ReDoc — clean API reference |
| `http://127.0.0.1:8000/health` | Liveness probe (JSON) |

### Quick smoke-test with curl

```bash
# Health check
curl http://127.0.0.1:8000/health

# Configure LLM and test connection
curl -s -X POST http://127.0.0.1:8000/api/v1/config/llm \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"provider":"dlh","model_name":"google/gemma-4-31b-it","dlh_base_url":"http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1","dlh_api_key":"EMPTY"}'

# Ask a compliance question
curl -s -X POST http://127.0.0.1:8000/api/v1/compliance/orchestrate \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"question":"How many KYC non-compliant accounts are there?"}' | python -m json.tool
```

### Stop the server

Press **Ctrl + C** in the terminal.

---

## Interface 2 — Full UI (`run_ui.py`)

`run_ui.py` is a **self-contained HTML chat UI** served by Python's built-in
HTTP server. It proxies all `/api/v1/...` calls to the FastAPI backend.

No extra dependencies — it uses only the Python standard library.

### Steps

#### Terminal 1 — start the backend

```bash
python application/main.py
```

Wait for the log line:
```
INFO:     Application startup complete.
```

#### Terminal 2 — start the UI server

**Linux / macOS**
```bash
python application/run_ui.py
```

**Windows CMD**
```cmd
python application\run_ui.py
```

Your browser opens automatically at **`http://127.0.0.1:5001`**.

### Custom ports

```bash
# Linux / macOS
CB_UI_PORT=6000 CB_BACKEND_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8000 python application/run_ui.py

# Windows CMD
set CB_UI_PORT=6000
set CB_BACKEND_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8000
python application\run_ui.py

# Windows PowerShell
$env:CB_UI_PORT="6000"
$env:CB_BACKEND_URL="http://127.0.0.1:8000"
python application\run_ui.py
```

### Using `Launcher.py` (both processes in one command)

`Launcher.py` starts the backend and the UI server together. Edit the
`PROJECT_ROOT`, `BACKEND_PORT`, and `UI_PORT` constants at the top of the
file to match your environment, then run:

```bash
python application/Launcher.py
```

Press **Ctrl + C** to stop both processes cleanly.

---

## Interface 3 — Jupyter Notebook (`notebook.ipynb`)

The notebook is a **Jupyter-based client** that talks to the running FastAPI
backend over HTTP. It provides:

- Single-question queries with formatted output
- An interactive widget-based chat (ipywidgets)
- Batch queries with a summary table
- CSV export of results
- Raw JSON API explorer

### Prerequisites

```bash
pip install notebook ipywidgets
jupyter nbextension enable --py widgetsnbextension
```

### Steps

#### Terminal 1 — start the backend

```bash
python application/main.py
```

#### Terminal 2 — open the notebook

```bash
jupyter notebook application/notebook.ipynb
```

Or with JupyterLab:

```bash
pip install jupyterlab
jupyter lab application/notebook.ipynb
```

### Run the cells in order

| Cell | Purpose |
|------|---------|
| **1** | Install / verify `requests`, `ipywidgets`, `pandas` |
| **2** | Set `BACKEND_URL` and `LLM_CONFIG` — **edit this cell** |
| **3** | Load HTTP + display helper functions |
| **4** | Health check — confirms backend is reachable |
| **5** | Configure LLM provider (POST `/api/v1/config/llm`) |
| **6** | Ask a single question — edit `QUESTION` |
| **7** | Interactive widget chat loop |
| **8** | Batch run a list of questions |
| **9** | Export results to CSV |
| **10** | Raw JSON response explorer |
| **11** | Sample questions reference table |

### Key configuration (Cell 2)

```python
BACKEND_URL = "http://127.0.0.1:8000"   # must match main.py port

LLM_CONFIG = {
    "provider": "dlh",
    "model_name": "google/gemma-4-31b-it",
    "dlh_base_url": "http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1",
    "dlh_api_key": "EMPTY",
    "dlh_verify_ssl": False,
}
```

---

## Environment variables reference

| Variable | Default | Description |
|----------|---------|-------------|
| `DLH_BASE_URL` | `http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1` | DLH inference server URL |
| `DLH_API_KEY` | `EMPTY` | Bearer token for DLH endpoint |
| `DLH_VERIFY_SSL` | `false` | Verify TLS certs for DLH |
| `LOG_LEVEL` | `INFO` | Logging verbosity (`DEBUG`, `INFO`, `WARNING`) |
| `LOG_DIR` | `data/logs` | Directory for rotating log files |
| `HOST` | `0.0.0.0` | Bind address for FastAPI |
| `PORT` | `8000` | Port for FastAPI |
| `DEBUG` | `false` | Enable uvicorn hot-reload |
| `CB_BACKEND_URL` | `http://127.0.0.1:8000` | Backend URL used by `run_ui.py` |
| `CB_UI_PORT` | `5001` | Port for the UI server |
| `CDSW_APP_PORT` | — | CML/CDSW app port (overrides `CB_UI_PORT`) |

---

## API reference

All endpoints are prefixed with the backend base URL (default `http://127.0.0.1:8000`).

### `GET /health`

Liveness probe.

```json
{ "status": "ok", "version": "2.0.0" }
```

---

### `POST /api/v1/config/llm`

Configure and test the LLM connection. Rebuilds the LangGraph pipeline.

**Request body**
```json
{
  "provider": "dlh",
  "model_name": "google/gemma-4-31b-it",
  "dlh_base_url": "http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1",
  "dlh_api_key": "EMPTY",
  "dlh_verify_ssl": false
}
```

**Response**
```json
{ "success": true, "provider": "dlh", "message": "LLM connection successful." }
```

---

### `POST /api/v1/compliance/chat`

Simplified NL-to-SQL query. Returns SQL + rows only.

**Request body**
```json
{ "question": "How many KYC non-compliant accounts are there?" }
```

**Response**
```json
{
  "question_id": "uuid",
  "sql_query": "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM COMP_TBL WHERE KYC_FLG = 'N'",
  "df_output": [{"COUNT(*)": 312}],
  "result_columns": ["COUNT(*)"],
  "result_summary": "1 row returned.",
  "error": null
}
```

---

### `POST /api/v1/compliance/orchestrate`

Full agentic orchestration response. Includes agent stage trace.

**Request body** — same as `/chat`

**Response**
```json
{
  "question_id": "uuid",
  "interpreted_question": "How many KYC non-compliant accounts are there?",
  "needs_clarification": false,
  "clarification_question": null,
  "sql_query": "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM COMP_TBL WHERE KYC_FLG = 'N'",
  "df_output": [{"COUNT(*)": 312}],
  "result_columns": ["COUNT(*)"],
  "result_summary": "1 row returned.",
  "stages": [
    "intent_checker:ok",
    "schema_checker:db=COMP_REPORTS",
    "sql_writer_agent:llm_generated retry=0",
    "sql_reviewer:passed",
    "sql_validator:passed",
    "executor_agent:ok rows=1"
  ],
  "error": null
}
```

---

## Troubleshooting

### `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'genai'`

You are running from inside the `application/` folder. Always run from the
**project root**:

```bash
# ✗ wrong
cd application && python main.py

# ✓ correct
python application/main.py
```

### Backend starts but LLM calls fail

1. Check `DLH_BASE_URL` in your `.env` or in Cell 2 of the notebook.
2. Confirm the inference server is reachable:
   ```bash
   curl http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1/models
   ```
3. Use the `/api/v1/config/llm` endpoint (or Cell 5 of the notebook) to
   reconfigure without restarting.

### `Cannot connect to backend at http://127.0.0.1:8000`

The FastAPI backend is not running. Start it first:
```bash
python application/main.py
```

### `ipywidgets` buttons are unresponsive

```bash
jupyter nbextension enable --py widgetsnbextension
# then restart the Jupyter server
```

### Port already in use

```bash
# Linux / macOS — find and kill the process on port 8000
lsof -ti:8000 | xargs kill -9

# Windows CMD
netstat -ano | findstr :8000
taskkill /PID <pid> /F
```



####################################################################################################
FILE: application\main.py
####################################################################################################



"""
application/main.py — FastAPI backend for the Compliance Chatbot v2.

Run directly from the project root (Linux / macOS / Windows CMD / PowerShell):

    python application/main.py
    python -m application.main          # alternative

Or via uvicorn explicitly:

    uvicorn application.main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8000

Wires together:
  - LangGraph NL2SQL agentic pipeline  (genai/nl2sql_agent/)
  - ComplianceProcessor                (genai/core/)
  - Static assets and CORS middleware

Endpoints:
  GET  /health                        — liveness probe
  POST /api/v1/config/llm             — configure & test LLM connection
  POST /api/v1/compliance/chat        — simplified NL-to-SQL chat response
  POST /api/v1/compliance/orchestrate — full agentic orchestration response
  GET  /                              — serve index.html SPA shell
"""

# ── Ensure the project root is on sys.path so both
#    `python application/main.py` and `python -m application.main` work. ──────
import sys
from pathlib import Path

_PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
if str(_PROJECT_ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(_PROJECT_ROOT))
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

from collections.abc import AsyncGenerator
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager

from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Request, status
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from fastapi.responses import FileResponse, JSONResponse
from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles

from genai.core.exceptions import ComplianceBotError
from genai.core.model import ModelLoaderService
from genai.core.processor import ComplianceProcessor
from genai.core.schemas import (
    ComplianceChatRequest,
    ComplianceChatResponse,
    ComplianceOrchestrationResponse,
    HealthResponse,
    LLMConnectionRequest,
    LLMConnectionResponse,
)
from genai.nl2sql_agent.graph import build_nl2sql_graph
from utils.config import ModelSettings, get_settings
from utils.logger import get_logger

LOGGER = get_logger("compliance_bot.application")

_model_service: ModelLoaderService | None = None
_compliance_processor: ComplianceProcessor | None = None


def _build_processor(model_settings: ModelSettings) -> tuple[ModelLoaderService, ComplianceProcessor]:
    """
    Construct a ``ModelLoaderService``, build the LangGraph pipeline, and
    return both the service and the compliance processor.
    """
    model_service = ModelLoaderService(settings=model_settings)
    model_service.load()

    db_path = get_settings().database.db_path
    compiled_graph = build_nl2sql_graph(
        model_service=model_service,
        db_path=db_path,
    )
    processor = ComplianceProcessor(compiled_graph=compiled_graph)
    return model_service, processor


@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI) -> AsyncGenerator[None, None]:
    """FastAPI lifespan handler — startup / shutdown."""
    global _model_service, _compliance_processor  # pylint: disable=global-statement
    settings = get_settings()
    LOGGER.info("Starting %s ...", settings.application_name)

    try:
        _model_service, _compliance_processor = _build_processor(settings.model)
    except ComplianceBotError:
        LOGGER.exception("Model loading failed — API will return 503 on inference")

    yield

    LOGGER.info("Shutting down %s ...", settings.application_name)
    if _model_service and _model_service.is_loaded():
        _model_service.unload()


settings = get_settings()
app = FastAPI(
    title=settings.application_name,
    description=(
        "LangGraph-based agentic NL-to-SQL compliance query engine for banking "
        "term-deposit products.  Six specialised agents collaborate to analyse "
        "intent, retrieve schema, generate, review, validate, and execute SQL queries."
    ),
    version="2.0.0",
    docs_url="/docs",
    redoc_url="/redoc",
    lifespan=lifespan,
)

app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=["*"],
    allow_credentials=True,
    allow_methods=["GET", "POST"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)

_assets_dir = _PROJECT_ROOT / "assets"
if _assets_dir.exists():
    app.mount("/assets", StaticFiles(directory=str(_assets_dir)), name="assets")


@app.exception_handler(ComplianceBotError)
async def compliance_bot_exception_handler(
    _request: Request,
    exc: ComplianceBotError,
) -> JSONResponse:
    """Map domain errors to a structured 500 JSON response."""
    LOGGER.error("Domain error: %s", exc.message)
    return JSONResponse(
        status_code=status.HTTP_500_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
        content={"error": exc.message},
    )


@app.get("/", include_in_schema=False)
async def index() -> FileResponse:
    """Serve the single-page application shell."""
    # Prefer the HTML file inside extra/ (original location), fall back to root.
    index_path = _PROJECT_ROOT / "extra" / "index.html"
    if not index_path.exists():
        index_path = _PROJECT_ROOT / "index.html"
    return FileResponse(str(index_path))


@app.get(
    "/health",
    response_model=HealthResponse,
    summary="Liveness probe",
    tags=["Infrastructure"],
)
async def health_check() -> HealthResponse:
    """Return service health status."""
    return HealthResponse(status="ok", version="2.0.0")


@app.post(
    "/api/v1/config/llm",
    response_model=LLMConnectionResponse,
    tags=["Configuration"],
    summary="Configure and test LLM connection",
)
async def configure_llm(request: LLMConnectionRequest) -> LLMConnectionResponse:
    """Reconfigure the active LLM provider and rebuild the LangGraph pipeline."""
    global _model_service, _compliance_processor  # pylint: disable=global-statement

    base_settings = get_settings().model.model_copy(deep=True)
    candidate_settings = base_settings.model_copy(
        update={
            "provider": "dlh",
            "model_name": request.model_name or base_settings.model_name,
            "dlh_base_url": request.dlh_base_url or base_settings.dlh_base_url,
            "dlh_api_key": request.dlh_api_key or base_settings.dlh_api_key,
            "dlh_verify_ssl": request.dlh_verify_ssl,
        }
    )

    model_service, processor = _build_processor(candidate_settings)
    model_service.test_connection()
    _model_service = model_service
    _compliance_processor = processor

    return LLMConnectionResponse(
        success=True,
        provider=candidate_settings.provider,
        message="LLM connection successful. LangGraph pipeline is ready.",
    )


@app.post(
    "/api/v1/compliance/chat",
    response_model=ComplianceChatResponse,
    summary="Natural-language compliance query (simplified response)",
    tags=["Compliance"],
    status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK,
)
async def compliance_chat(request: ComplianceChatRequest) -> ComplianceChatResponse:
    """Run the NL2SQL agentic pipeline and return a simplified response."""
    if _compliance_processor is None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
            detail="Compliance processor is not initialised. Please retry in a few seconds.",
        )
    LOGGER.info("Received chat request — question_id=%s", request.question_id)
    return _compliance_processor.process_query(request)


@app.post(
    "/api/v1/compliance/orchestrate",
    response_model=ComplianceOrchestrationResponse,
    summary="Agentic NL2SQL compliance query (full orchestration response)",
    tags=["Compliance"],
    status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK,
)
async def compliance_orchestrate(request: ComplianceChatRequest) -> ComplianceOrchestrationResponse:
    """Run the full LangGraph NL2SQL pipeline and return agent-level detail."""
    if _compliance_processor is None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
            detail="Compliance processor is not initialised. Please retry in a few seconds.",
        )
    return _compliance_processor.process_query_with_orchestration(request)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    import argparse

    import uvicorn

    _parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Compliance Bot FastAPI backend")
    _parser.add_argument("--host", default=None, help="Bind host (overrides .env HOST)")
    _parser.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=None, help="Bind port (overrides .env PORT)")
    _args = _parser.parse_args()

    _settings = get_settings()
    _host = _args.host or _settings.host
    _port = _args.port or _settings.port

    LOGGER.info("Binding on %s:%d — run from project root, not from application/", _host, _port)
    uvicorn.run(
        "application.main:app",
        host=_host,
        port=_port,
        reload=_settings.debug,
        log_level=_settings.log_level.lower(),
    )

   "source": [
    "## Cell 3 — Helper utilities"
   ]
  },
  {
   "cell_type": "code",
   "execution_count": null,
   "id": "10185d26023b46108eb7d9f57d49d2b3",
   "metadata": {},
   "outputs": [],
   "source": [
    "import json\n",
    "\n",
    "import pandas as pd\n",
    "import requests\n",
    "from IPython.display import Markdown, display\n",
    "\n",
    "# ── Low-level HTTP helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
    "\n",
    "def _post(path: str, payload: dict, timeout: int = 120) -> dict:\n",
    "    \"\"\"POST to the backend and return the parsed JSON body.\"\"\"\n",
    "    url = f\"{BACKEND_URL}{path}\"\n",
    "    try:\n",
    "        resp = requests.post(url, json=payload, timeout=timeout)\n",
    "        resp.raise_for_status()\n",
    "        return resp.json()\n",
    "    except requests.exceptions.ConnectionError:\n",
    "        return {\"error\": f\"Cannot connect to backend at {BACKEND_URL}.\\n\"\n",
    "                         f\"Start it with: python application/main.py\"}\n",
    "    except requests.exceptions.HTTPError as exc:\n",
    "        try:\n",
    "            body = exc.response.json()\n",
    "        except Exception:\n",
    "            body = {\"error\": str(exc)}\n",
    "        return body\n",
    "    except Exception as exc:\n",
    "        return {\"error\": str(exc)}\n",
    "\n",
    "def _get(path: str, timeout: int = 10) -> dict:\n",
    "    \"\"\"GET from the backend and return the parsed JSON body.\"\"\"\n",
    "    url = f\"{BACKEND_URL}{path}\"\n",
    "    try:\n",
    "        resp = requests.get(url, timeout=timeout)\n",
    "        resp.raise_for_status()\n",
    "        return resp.json()\n",
    "    except requests.exceptions.ConnectionError:\n",
    "        return {\"error\": f\"Cannot connect to backend at {BACKEND_URL}.\\n\"\n",
    "                         f\"Start it with: python application/main.py\"}\n",
    "    except Exception as exc:\n",
    "        return {\"error\": str(exc)}\n",
    "\n",
    "# ── Display helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
    "\n",
    "def _print_stages(stages: list):\n",
    "    if not stages:\n",
    "        return\n",
    "    pills = \" → \".join(f\"`{s}`\" for s in stages)\n",
    "    display(Markdown(f\"**Agent stages:** {pills}\"))\n",
    "\n",
    "def _print_result(data: dict):\n",
    "    \"\"\"Pretty-print a /orchestrate response.\"\"\"\n",
    "    if data.get(\"error\"):\n",
    "        display(Markdown(f\"❌ **Error:** {data['error']}\"))\n",
    "        return\n",
    "\n",
    "    _print_stages(data.get(\"stages\", []))\n",
    "\n",
    "    if data.get(\"needs_clarification\"):\n",
    "        display(Markdown(f\"❓ **Clarification needed:** {data.get('clarification_question')}\"))\n",
    "        return\n",
    "\n",
    "    if data.get(\"sql_query\"):\n",
    "        display(Markdown(f\"**Generated SQL**\\n```sql\\n{data['sql_query']}\\n```\"))\n",
    "\n",
    "    if data.get(\"result_summary\"):\n",
    "        display(Markdown(f\"**Summary:** {data['result_summary']}\"))\n",
    "\n",
    "    rows = data.get(\"df_output\") or []\n",
    "    cols = data.get(\"result_columns\") or []\n",
    "    if rows:\n",
    "        df = pd.DataFrame(rows, columns=cols or None)\n",
    "        display(Markdown(f\"**Rows returned:** {len(df)}\"))\n",
    "        display(df)\n",
    "    else:\n",
    "        display(Markdown(\"*No rows returned.*\"))\n",
    "\n",
    "print(\"Helpers loaded ✔\")"
   ]
  },
  {
   "cell_type": "markdown",
   "id": "8763a12b2bbd4a93a75aff182afb95dc",
   "metadata": {},
   "source": [

    "## Cell 7 — Interactive chat loop (ipywidgets)\n",
    "\n",
    "A lightweight widget-based chat. Type your question and press **Send**.  \n",
    "Requires `ipywidgets` and the `widgetsnbextension` to be enabled:\n",
    "\n",
    "```bash\n",
    "jupyter nbextension enable --py widgetsnbextension\n",
    "```"
   ]
  },
  {
   "cell_type": "code",
   "execution_count": null,
   "id": "b43b363d81ae4b689946ece5c682cd59",
   "metadata": {},
   "outputs": [],
   "source": [
    "import ipywidgets as widgets\n",
    "from IPython.display import clear_output\n",
    "\n",
    "# ── UI components ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n",
    "question_box = widgets.Textarea(\n",
    "    placeholder=\"Type your compliance question here…\",\n",
    "    layout=widgets.Layout(width=\"100%\", height=\"70px\"),\n",
    ")\n",
    "send_btn = widgets.Button(\n",
    "    description=\"Send\",\n",
    "    button_style=\"primary\",\n",
    "    tooltip=\"Send question to Compliance Bot\",\n",
    "    icon=\"paper-plane\",\n",
    ")\n",
    "clear_btn = widgets.Button(\n",
    "    description=\"Clear chat\",\n",
    "    button_style=\"warning\",\n",
    "    tooltip=\"Clear conversation history\",\n",
    "    icon=\"trash\",\n",
    ")\n",
    "out = widgets.Output()\n",
    "status_label = widgets.Label(value=\"\")\n",
    "\n",
    "_history: list = []\n",
    "\n",
    "def _render_history():\n",
    "    for role, text, data in _history:\n",
    "        if role == \"user\":\n",
    "            display(Markdown(f\"---\\n**You:** {text}\"))\n",
    "        else:\n",
    "            display(Markdown(\"**Bot:**\"))\n",
    "            _print_result(data)\n",
    "\n",
    "def on_send(btn):\n",
    "    question = question_box.value.strip()\n",
    "    if not question:\n",
    "        return\n",
    "    status_label.value = \"⏳ Waiting for response…\"\n",
    "    send_btn.disabled = True\n",
    "    question_box.value = \"\"\n",
    "\n",
    "    data = _post(\"/api/v1/compliance/orchestrate\", {\"question\": question})\n",
    "    _history.append((\"user\", question, None))\n",
    "    _history.append((\"bot\", \"\", data))\n",
    "\n",
    "    with out:\n",
    "        clear_output(wait=True)\n",
    "        _render_history()\n",
    "\n",
    "    status_label.value = \"✅ Done\"\n",
    "    send_btn.disabled = False\n",
    "\n",
    "def on_clear(btn):\n",
    "    _history.clear()\n",
    "    with out:\n",
    "        clear_output()\n",
    "    status_label.value = \"Chat cleared.\"\n",
    "\n",
    "send_btn.on_click(on_send)\n",
    "clear_btn.on_click(on_clear)\n",
    "\n",
    "btn_row = widgets.HBox([send_btn, clear_btn, status_label])\n",
    "ui = widgets.VBox([\n",
    "    widgets.HTML(\"<h3>💬 Compliance Bot Chat</h3>\"),\n",
    "    question_box,\n",
    "    btn_row,\n",
    "    out,\n",
    "])\n",
    "display(ui)"
   ]
  },
  {

    "| # | Question | Expected behaviour |\n",

    "| 4 | `What is the average balance for Thane branch?` | AVG aggregate |\n",
    "| 5 | `How many accounts are maturing next month?` | Date-range filter on `MTRTY_DT` |\n",
    "| 6 | `Top 10 accounts by end-of-day balance` | ORDER BY + LIMIT |\n",
    "| 7 | `Count accounts by product name` | GROUP BY aggregate |\n",
    "| 8 | `Who?` | Triggers clarification flow |\n",
    "\n",

    "- Re-run **Cell 5** to switch LLM providers without restarting the notebook.\n",

FILE: application\run_ui.py

"""
run_ui.py — Self-contained UI server for Compliance Bot (LangGraph).

Run (from the project root, after starting main.py in another terminal):

    python application/run_ui.py

What it does:
  • Embeds the full single-page app as a Python string (no separate index.html needed).
  • Starts Python's built-in HTTP server on port 5001 (override: CB_UI_PORT env var).
  • Proxies every /api/v1/... request to the FastAPI backend
    (default http://127.0.0.1:8000 — override: CB_BACKEND_URL env var).
  • Renders live LangGraph six-node stage traces returned by /api/v1/compliance/orchestrate.
  • Opens the browser automatically.

No extra dependencies beyond the Python standard library.
"""

import json
import os
import sys
import threading
import urllib.error
import urllib.request
import webbrowser
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, HTTPServer
from pathlib import Path

# ── Ensure project root is importable when run directly ──────────────────────
_PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
if str(_PROJECT_ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(_PROJECT_ROOT))
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

# Configuration
UI_PORT: int = int(os.environ.get("CDSW_APP_PORT", os.getenv("CB_UI_PORT", "5001")))
BACKEND_URL: str = os.getenv("CB_BACKEND_URL", "http://127.0.0.1:8000").rstrip("/")

_THIS_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
_FONTS_DIR = _THIS_DIR / "fonts" if (_THIS_DIR / "fonts").is_dir() else _THIS_DIR / "application" / "fonts"

  <script>
    const appShell = document.getElementById('appShell');
    const sidebar = document.getElementById('sidebar');
    const resizeHandle = document.getElementById('resizeHandle');
    const collapseBtn = document.getElementById('collapseBtn');
    const hamburgerBtn = document.getElementById('hamburgerBtn');
    const pageTitle = document.getElementById('pageTitle');
    const pageSubtitle = document.getElementById('pageSubtitle');
    const navItems = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item'));
    const pages = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.page'));
    const statusBox = document.getElementById('statusBox');
    const chatWindow = document.getElementById('chatWindow');
    const questionInput = document.getElementById('questionInput');
    const savedConfigPreview = document.getElementById('savedConfigPreview');

    const STORAGE_KEYS = {
      sidebarWidth: 'cb_sidebar_width',
      sidebarCollapsed: 'cb_sidebar_collapsed',
      activePage: 'cb_active_page',
      llmConfig: 'cb_llm_config'
    };

    const PAGE_META = {
      'llm-config': { title: 'LLM Configuration', subtitle: 'Configure the LLM provider — the LangGraph pipeline rebuilds automatically on save.' },
      chat: { title: 'Chat', subtitle: 'Six LangGraph nodes: Intent Checker → Schema Checker → SQL Writer (LLM) → SQL Review → SQL Validator → Executor.' }
    };

    // Maps a LangGraph stage string prefix to the display name of the stage pill.
    // Prefixes must match the strings emitted by each agent node exactly:
    //   intent_checker.py    → "intent_checker:…"
    //   schema_checker.py    → "schema_checker:…"
    //   sql_writer_agent.py  → "sql_writer_agent:…"
    //   sql_reviewer.py      → "sql_reviewer:…"
    //   sql_validator.py     → "sql_validator:…"
    //   executor_agent.py    → "executor_agent:…"
    const STAGE_PREFIX_MAP = [
      { prefix: 'intent_checker',   label: 'Intent Checker'  },
      { prefix: 'schema_checker',   label: 'Schema Checker'  },
      { prefix: 'sql_writer_agent', label: 'SQL Writer'      },
      { prefix: 'sql_reviewer',     label: 'SQL Review'      },
      { prefix: 'sql_validator',    label: 'SQL Validator'   },
      { prefix: 'executor_agent',   label: 'Executor'        },
      { prefix: 'clarify_node',     label: 'Clarification'   },
    ];

    /**
     * Highlight the stage pills that appear in the live LangGraph stage trace.
     * Each pill's data-prefix attribute is compared against every stage string
     * returned by the orchestrate endpoint; a match lights the pill up in accent blue.
     */
    function updateStagePills(stages) {
      const trail = document.getElementById('stageTrail');
      if (!trail) return;
      const pills = trail.querySelectorAll('.stage');
      pills.forEach(pill => {
        pill.style.background = '';
        pill.style.color = '';
        pill.style.borderColor = '';
        pill.style.fontWeight = '';
      });
      STAGE_PREFIX_MAP.forEach(({ prefix, label }, idx) => {
        const matched = stages.some(s => s.startsWith(prefix));
        if (!matched) return;
        const pill = pills[idx];
        if (!pill) return;
        pill.style.background = '#2563eb';
        pill.style.color = '#ffffff';
        pill.style.borderColor = '#1d4ed8';
        pill.style.fontWeight = '600';
      });
    }

    function setSidebarWidth(width) {
      const min = 220;
      const max = 420;
      const clamped = Math.min(max, Math.max(min, width));
      document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--sidebar-width', `${clamped}px`);
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.sidebarWidth, String(clamped));
    }

    function setCollapsed(collapsed) {
      appShell.classList.toggle('sidebar-collapsed', collapsed);
      collapseBtn.textContent = collapsed ? '▶' : '◀';
      collapseBtn.setAttribute('aria-label', collapsed ? 'Expand sidebar' : 'Collapse sidebar');
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.sidebarCollapsed, String(collapsed));
    }

    function saveConfigLocally(config) {
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.llmConfig, JSON.stringify(config));
      savedConfigPreview.textContent = JSON.stringify(config, null, 2);
    }

    function loadConfigLocally() {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.llmConfig);
      if (!saved) {
        savedConfigPreview.textContent = 'No saved configuration yet.';
        return null;
      }
      const config = JSON.parse(saved);
      document.getElementById('modelName').value = config.model_name || 'google/gemma-4-31b-it';
      document.getElementById('dlhBaseUrl').value = config.dlh_base_url || '';
      document.getElementById('dlhApiKey').value = config.dlh_api_key || '';
      savedConfigPreview.textContent = JSON.stringify(config, null, 2);
      return config;
    }

    function renderMarkdown(text) {
      const escaped = text
        .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
        .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
        .replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
      return escaped
        .replace(/```([\s\S]*?)```/g, '<pre class="code-block">$1</pre>')
        .replace(/\*\*(.*?)\*\*/g, '<strong>$1</strong>')
        .replace(/`([^`]+)`/g, '<code>$1</code>')
        .replace(/\n/g, '<br>');
    }

    function addMessage(text, role, meta, persist = true) {
      const msg = document.createElement('div');
      msg.className = `msg ${role}`;
      msg.innerHTML = role === 'bot' ? renderMarkdown(text) : text;
      chatWindow.appendChild(msg);

      if (meta) {
        const metaBox = document.createElement('div');
        metaBox.className = 'meta markdown';
        metaBox.innerHTML = meta;
        chatWindow.appendChild(metaBox);
      }

      chatWindow.scrollTop = chatWindow.scrollHeight;

      if (persist) {
        chatWindow.dataset.hasConversation = 'true';
      }
    }

    function clearChat() {
      chatWindow.innerHTML = '';
      delete chatWindow.dataset.hasConversation;
      updateStagePills([]); // reset all pills to default (unlit) state
    }

    function setActivePage(pageId) {
      navItems.forEach((item) => item.classList.toggle('active', item.dataset.page === pageId));
      pages.forEach((page) => page.classList.toggle('active', page.dataset.page === pageId));
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.activePage, pageId);
      pageTitle.textContent = PAGE_META[pageId].title;
      pageSubtitle.textContent = PAGE_META[pageId].subtitle;
      if (window.innerWidth <= 920) {
        appShell.classList.remove('mobile-sidebar-open');
      }
    }

    async function connectProvider() {
      const btn = document.getElementById('connectBtn');
      btn.disabled = true;

      // Show spinning dots while connecting
      statusBox.className = 'status testing';
      statusBox.innerHTML =
        '<div class="status-dots"><span></span><span></span><span></span></div>' +
        '<span>Testing connection\u2026</span>';

      const payload = {
        provider: 'dlh',
        model_name: document.getElementById('modelName').value,
        dlh_base_url: document.getElementById('dlhBaseUrl').value,
        dlh_api_key: document.getElementById('dlhApiKey').value,
      };

      try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/v1/config/llm', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify(payload)
        });
        const data = await response.json();
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || data.detail || 'Connection failed');
        statusBox.className = 'status success';
        statusBox.textContent = data.message;
        saveConfigLocally(payload);
        addMessage(`Connected to ${data.provider}. LangGraph pipeline is ready.`, 'bot');
        checkBackendHealth();
        setActivePage('chat');
      } catch (error) {
        statusBox.className = 'status error';
        statusBox.textContent = error.message;
      } finally {
        btn.disabled = false;
      }
    }

    function showTypingIndicator() {
      const el = document.createElement('div');
      el.className = 'typing-indicator';
      el.id = 'typingIndicator';
      el.innerHTML = '<span></span><span></span><span></span>';
      chatWindow.appendChild(el);
      chatWindow.scrollTop = chatWindow.scrollHeight;
    }

    function hideTypingIndicator() {
      const el = document.getElementById('typingIndicator');
      if (el) el.remove();
    }

    // Render text as markdown (bold + code + pre + newlines).
    function formatNoticeText(text) {
      return text
        .replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
        .replace(/\*\*(.*?)\*\*/g, '<strong>$1</strong>')
        .replace(/`([^`]+)`/g, '<code>$1</code>')
        .replace(/\n/g, '<br>');
    }

    // Append a styled notice card (blocked = red, clarify = blue) to the chat.
    function addNotice(text, type) {
      const el = document.createElement('div');
      el.className = `msg-notice ${type}`;
      el.innerHTML = formatNoticeText(text);
      chatWindow.appendChild(el);
      chatWindow.scrollTop = chatWindow.scrollHeight;
      chatWindow.dataset.hasConversation = 'true';
    }

    async function sendMessage() {
      const question = questionInput.value.trim();
      if (!question) return;

      const sendBtn = document.getElementById('sendBtn');
      addMessage(question, 'user');
      questionInput.value = '';
      sendBtn.disabled = true;
      sendBtn.textContent = 'Sending\u2026';
      showTypingIndicator();

      try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/v1/compliance/orchestrate', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ question })
        });
        const data = await response.json();
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || data.detail || 'Chat failed');

        // Update stage pills with live LangGraph trace.
        updateStagePills(data.stages || []);

        const stageTrace = (data.stages || []).join(' → ');
        const stageHtml = `<div style="font-size:12px;color:#57606a;margin-bottom:10px;word-break:break-all">${stageTrace || '—'}</div>`;

        // ── Clarification requested (vague or ambiguous question) ──────────
        if (data.needs_clarification) {
          hideTypingIndicator();
          addNotice(data.clarification_question, 'clarify');
          return;
        }

        // ── Pipeline error (blocked keyword, max retries, etc.) ────────────
        if (data.error) {
          hideTypingIndicator();
          addNotice(data.error, 'blocked');
          return;
        }

        // ── Successful query result ────────────────────────────────────────
        const rowCount = Array.isArray(data.df_output) ? data.df_output.length : 0;
        const summary  = data.result_summary || `${rowCount} row(s) returned.`;
        const resultsJson = JSON.stringify(data.df_output || [], null, 2);
        const encodedData = encodeURIComponent(resultsJson);
        hideTypingIndicator();
        addMessage(
          'Query completed successfully.',
          'bot',
          `<strong>Agent Trace</strong>${stageHtml}` +
          `<strong>Summary</strong><br>${summary.replace(/\*\*(.*?)\*\*/g,'<strong>$1</strong>').replace(/`([^`]+)`/g,'<code>$1</code>')}<br><br>` +
          `<strong>SQL</strong><pre class="code-block">${(data.sql_query || 'N/A').replace(/</g,'&lt;')}</pre>` +
          `<br><strong>Rows returned:</strong> ${rowCount}<br><br>` +
          `<strong>Results</strong><pre class="code-block" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">${resultsJson}</pre>` +
          `<button class="csv-download-btn" data-csv="${encodedData}" onclick="downloadCsv(this)">&#8595; Download as CSV</button>`
        );
      } catch (error) {
        hideTypingIndicator();
        addNotice('An unexpected error occurred. Please try again.', 'blocked');
      } finally {
        sendBtn.disabled = false;
        sendBtn.textContent = 'Send';
      }
    }

    function downloadCsv(btn) {
      let rows;
      try { rows = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(btn.dataset.csv)); } catch (_) { return; }
      if (!Array.isArray(rows) || rows.length === 0) { alert('No data to download.'); return; }
      const headers = Object.keys(rows[0]);
      const escape  = v => {
        const s = v === null || v === undefined ? '' : String(v);
        return s.includes(',') || s.includes('"') || s.includes('\n')
          ? `"${s.replace(/"/g, '""')}"` : s;
      };
      const csvContent = [
        headers.map(escape).join(','),
        ...rows.map(r => headers.map(h => escape(r[h])).join(','))
      ].join('\r\n');
      const blob = new Blob([csvContent], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' });
      const url  = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const a    = document.createElement('a');
      a.href     = url;
      a.download = `results_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0,19).replace(/[:T]/g,'-')}.csv`;
      a.click();
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    }

    function getConversationEntries() {
      return Array.from(chatWindow.querySelectorAll('.msg, .meta'));
    }

    function copyChat() {
      const transcript = getConversationEntries()
        .map((entry) => entry.innerText.trim())
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join('\n\n');
      navigator.clipboard.writeText(transcript || 'No conversation available.');
    }

    function exportChat() {
      const transcript = getConversationEntries()
        .map((entry) => entry.innerText.trim())
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join('\n\n');
      const blob = new Blob([transcript || 'No conversation available.'], { type: 'text/plain' });
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const a = document.createElement('a');
      a.href = url;
      a.download = 'conversation.txt';
      a.click();
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    }

    navItems.forEach((item) => {
      item.addEventListener('click', () => setActivePage(item.dataset.page));
      item.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
        if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') {
          event.preventDefault();
          setActivePage(item.dataset.page);
        }
      });
    });

    document.getElementById('connectBtn').addEventListener('click', connectProvider);
    document.getElementById('loadConfigBtn').addEventListener('click', loadConfigLocally);
    document.getElementById('sendBtn').addEventListener('click', sendMessage);
    document.getElementById('clearBtn').addEventListener('click', clearChat);
    document.getElementById('copyChatBtn').addEventListener('click', copyChat);
    document.getElementById('exportChatBtn').addEventListener('click', exportChat);

    questionInput.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
      if (event.key === 'Enter' && !event.shiftKey) {
        event.preventDefault();
        sendMessage();
      }
    });

    collapseBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      setCollapsed(!appShell.classList.contains('sidebar-collapsed'));
    });

    hamburgerBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      appShell.classList.toggle('mobile-sidebar-open');
    });

    let resizing = false;
    resizeHandle.addEventListener('mousedown', () => {
      if (appShell.classList.contains('sidebar-collapsed') || window.innerWidth <= 920) return;
      resizing = true;
      document.body.style.cursor = 'col-resize';
    });

    document.addEventListener('mousemove', (event) => {
      if (!resizing) return;
      setSidebarWidth(event.clientX);
    });

    document.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
      if (!resizing) return;
      resizing = false;
      document.body.style.cursor = 'default';
    });

    // ── Backend health polling ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    const backendStatus = document.getElementById('backendStatus');
    const backendLabel  = document.getElementById('backendLabel');

    async function checkBackendHealth() {
      try {
        const res = await fetch('/health', { method: 'GET', signal: AbortSignal.timeout(4000) });
        if (res.ok) {
          backendStatus.className = 'backend-status alive';
          backendLabel.textContent = 'Backend Active';
        } else {
          throw new Error('non-ok');
        }
      } catch (_) {
        backendStatus.className = 'backend-status dead';
        backendLabel.textContent = 'Backend Offline';
      }
    }

    checkBackendHealth();
    setInterval(checkBackendHealth, 8000);
    // ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    // ── One-time migration: remove ICA fields from any previously saved config ──
    (function migrateStoredConfig() {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.llmConfig);
      if (!raw) return;
      try {
        const cfg = JSON.parse(raw);
        const { ica_base_url, ica_api_key, ica_namespace, ica_send_temperature, ...rest } = cfg;
        rest.provider = 'dlh';
        localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.llmConfig, JSON.stringify(rest));
      } catch (_) {}
    })();
    // ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    const savedWidth = Number(localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.sidebarWidth) || '280');
    setSidebarWidth(savedWidth);
    setCollapsed(localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.sidebarCollapsed) === 'true');
    const savedPage = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.activePage) || 'llm-config';
    setActivePage(savedPage);
    loadConfigLocally();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
"""


class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    """Serve the UI and proxy /api/v1/* to the FastAPI backend."""

    # Silence the default per-request access log lines.
    def log_message(self, fmt: str, *args) -> None:
        pass

    def _send(self, code: int, content_type: str, body: bytes) -> None:
        try:
            self.send_response(code)
            self.send_header("Content-Type", content_type)
            self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
            self.end_headers()
            self.wfile.write(body)
        except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionAbortedError, ConnectionResetError):
            # Client closed the connection before the response was sent
            # (e.g. browser tab closed, LLM took too long).  Nothing to do.
            pass

    # Serve the HTML page, static assets and proxy GET API calls.
    def do_GET(self) -> None:
        path = self.path.split("?")[0]

        if path in ("/", "/index.html"):
            body = HTML.encode("utf-8")
            self._send(200, "text/html; charset=utf-8", body)
            return

        # Serve font files from application/fonts/
        if path.startswith("/fonts/"):
            file_path = (_FONTS_DIR / path[len("/fonts/"):]).resolve()
            # Prevent path traversal outside fonts dir
            if not str(file_path).startswith(str(_FONTS_DIR)):
                self._send(403, "text/plain", b"Forbidden")
                return
            if file_path.is_file():
                data = file_path.read_bytes()
                ct = "font/truetype" if file_path.suffix in (".ttf", ".otf") else "application/octet-stream"
                self._send(200, ct, data)
            else:
                print(f"[font] 404 — file not found: {file_path}")
                self._send(404, "text/plain", b"Not found")
            return

        # Proxy GET API calls and the backend health probe
        if path.startswith("/api/") or path == "/health":
            self._proxy_get(path)
            return

        self._send(404, "text/plain", b"Not found")

    def _proxy_get(self, path: str) -> None:
        try:
            url = BACKEND_URL + path
            with urllib.request.urlopen(url, timeout=15) as resp:
                body = resp.read()
                self._send(resp.status, resp.headers.get("Content-Type", "application/json"), body)
        except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionAbortedError, ConnectionResetError):
            pass  # client already disconnected — nothing to send
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            err = json.dumps({"error": str(exc)}).encode()
            self._send(502, "application/json", err)

    # Proxy all POST /api/* requests directly to the FastAPI backend.
    def do_POST(self) -> None:
        path = self.path.split("?")[0]
        if not path.startswith("/api/"):
            self._send(404, "text/plain", b"Not found")
            return

        length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", 0))
        raw_body = self.rfile.read(length) if length else b""

        target_url = BACKEND_URL + path
        req = urllib.request.Request(
            target_url,
            data=raw_body,
            headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"},
            method="POST",
        )
        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=120) as resp:
                body = resp.read()
                self._send(resp.status, resp.headers.get("Content-Type", "application/json"), body)
        except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionAbortedError, ConnectionResetError):
            pass  # client disconnected before response arrived — nothing to send
        except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
            body = exc.read()
            self._send(exc.code, exc.headers.get("Content-Type", "application/json"), body)
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            err = json.dumps({"error": str(exc)}).encode()
            self._send(502, "application/json", err)


def main() -> None:
    print("CDSW_APP_PORT =", os.getenv("CDSW_APP_PORT"))
    print("CDSW_READONLY_PORT = ", os.getenv("CDSW_READONLY_PORT"))
    print("CB_APP_PORT =", os.getenv("CB_APP_PORT"))
    print("UI_PORT =", UI_PORT)
    print("CDSW_PUBLIC_PORT =", os.getenv("CDSW_PUBLIC_PORT"))

    server = HTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", UI_PORT), Handler)
    url = f"http://127.0.0.1:{UI_PORT}"
    print(f"  Compliance Bot UI  →  {url}")
    print(f"  Backend proxy      →  {BACKEND_URL}")
    print("  Press Ctrl+C to stop.\n")

    # Open browser after a short delay so the server is ready
    threading.Timer(0.8, lambda: webbrowser.open(url)).start()

    try:
        server.serve_forever()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\nStopped.")
        sys.exit(0)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

"""
Synthetic data generator for COMP_TBL.

Creates the database file, creates the table if absent, and inserts
realistic term-deposit compliance records into
``data/comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db``.  Run from the project root:

    python data/seed_synthetic_data.py              # create + insert 2000 rows
    python data/seed_synthetic_data.py --reset      # drop all rows then insert
    python data/seed_synthetic_data.py --count 500  # custom row count
    python data/seed_synthetic_data.py --count 0    # create DB/table only, no rows

The script uses only the Python standard library — no external packages needed.

Modular structure
-----------------
  ensure_database(db_path)          → creates the .db file and parent directories
  ensure_table(conn)                → creates COMP_TBL if it does not exist
  reset_table(conn)                 → deletes all existing rows
  generate_rows(count, start_i)     → returns a list of synthetic row tuples
  insert_rows(conn, rows)           → bulk-inserts rows
  print_summary(conn, db_path, n)   → prints statistics after insert
  seed(db_path, count, reset)       → orchestrates all of the above
"""

import argparse
import random
import sqlite3
from datetime import date, timedelta
from pathlib import Path

_CREATE_TABLE_SQL = """
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS COMP_TBL (
    BRCD               TEXT,
    CUST_NBR           TEXT,
    CUST_NBR_CHKDGT    TEXT,
    ACCT_NBR           TEXT,
    ACCT_NBR_CHKDGT    TEXT,
    ACCT_BRNCH         TEXT,
    BRTH_DT            TEXT,
    AGE_YRS            INTEGER,
    SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG   INTEGER,
    KYC_FLG            TEXT,
    END_OF_DAY_BAL     REAL,
    ACCT_OPN_DT        TEXT,
    MTRTY_DT           TEXT,
    tenor_yr           INTEGER,
    tenor_day          INTEGER,
    MAT_DT             TEXT,
    NEW_EFFCTV_RT      REAL,
    PRDCT_ID           TEXT,
    PF_FAC_REPT        TEXT,
    PRODUCT_NAME       TEXT,
    BRANCHNAME         TEXT,
    CIRCLENAME         TEXT,
    CIRCLECD           TEXT,
    EXPECTED_EFFCTV_RT REAL,
    ISMOD              TEXT
)
"""

    ("P106", "FLEXI DEPOSIT PLAN"),
    ("P107", "TAX SAVER FIXED DEPOSIT"),
    ("P108", "SUPER SENIOR DEPOSIT"),
    ("P109", "SHORT TERM DEPOSIT 7-30D"),
    ("P110", "MEDIUM TERM DEPOSIT 180D-1YR"),
]


def ensure_database(db_path: Path) -> None:
    """
    Create the parent directory and SQLite database file if they do not exist.

    This is a no-op when both already exist — SQLite creates the file on
    first ``connect()`` so the function just guarantees the directory is there.

    Args:
        db_path: Absolute or relative path to the ``.db`` file.
    """
    db_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    existed = db_path.exists()
    sqlite3.connect(str(db_path)).close()
    if not existed:
        print(f"[create] Database file created: {db_path}")
    else:
        print(f"[ok]     Database file exists:  {db_path}")


def ensure_table(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
    """
    Create ``COMP_TBL`` with the full 25-column schema if it does not exist.

    If the table already exists but is missing required columns (stale schema),
    it is dropped and recreated so the seeder can always run safely.

    Args:
        conn: Open SQLite connection to the target database.
    """
    table_exists = conn.execute(
        "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='COMP_TBL'"
    ).fetchone()

    if table_exists:
        existing_cols: set[str] = {
            row[1] for row in conn.execute("PRAGMA table_info(COMP_TBL)").fetchall()
        }
        if not _REQUIRED_COLUMNS.issubset(existing_cols):
            conn.execute("DROP TABLE COMP_TBL")
            conn.commit()
            print("[migrate] COMP_TBL schema was stale — dropped and will be recreated.")
            table_exists = None  # fall through to CREATE

    if not table_exists:
        conn.execute(_CREATE_TABLE_SQL)
        conn.commit()
        print("[create] COMP_TBL created.")
    else:
        print("[ok]     COMP_TBL already exists with correct schema.")


def reset_table(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
    """
    Delete every row from ``COMP_TBL``.

    Args:
        conn: Open SQLite connection to the target database.
    """
    deleted: int = conn.execute("DELETE FROM COMP_TBL").rowcount
    conn.commit()
    print(f"[reset]  Deleted {deleted:,} existing rows from COMP_TBL.")


def _rand_date(start: date, end: date) -> date:
    """Return a random date between *start* and *end* (inclusive)."""
    return start + timedelta(days=random.randint(0, (end - start).days))


def _fmt(d: date) -> str:
    """Format a date as ISO-8601 string (YYYY-MM-DD)."""
    return d.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")


def _rand_rate(base: float) -> float:
    """Return a rate within ±0.75 pp of *base*, rounded to 2 dp."""
    return round(base + random.uniform(-0.75, 0.75), 2)


def _make_row(i: int) -> tuple:  # type: ignore[type-arg]
    """Generate one synthetic COMP_TBL row."""
    circle_name, circle_cd = random.choice(_CIRCLES)
    branch_name            = random.choice(_BRANCHES[circle_cd])
    prdct_id, product_name = random.choice(_PRODUCTS)

    age: int     = random.randint(18, 85)
    sr_flag: int = 1 if age >= 60 else 0
    dob: str     = _fmt(_rand_date(date(2025 - age - 1, 1, 1), date(2025 - age, 12, 31)))

    kyc: str        = "Y" if random.random() < (0.80 if sr_flag else 0.90) else "N"
    balance: float  = round(random.lognormvariate(11.5, 1.2), 2)
    balance         = max(10_000.0, min(5_000_000.0, balance))

    open_dt: date   = _rand_date(date(2005, 1, 1), date(2024, 6, 30))
    tenor_yr: int   = random.choice([1, 2, 3, 5, 7, 10, 15])
    tenor_day: int  = random.randint(0, 364)
    mat_dt_d: date  = open_dt + timedelta(days=tenor_yr * 365 + tenor_day)
    mat_dt: str     = _fmt(mat_dt_d)

    is_mod: str  = "YES" if random.random() < 0.15 else "NO"
    mtrty_dt: str = _fmt(mat_dt_d + timedelta(days=random.randint(-30, 30))) if is_mod == "YES" else mat_dt

    base_rate: float = 7.0 if sr_flag else 6.5
    new_rate: float  = _rand_rate(base_rate)
    exp_rate: float  = _rand_rate(base_rate + 0.25)
    pf_fac: str      = "Y" if random.random() < 0.30 else "N"
    brcd: str        = f"{random.randint(1, 999):03d}"
    acct_brnch: str  = f"M{random.randint(1, 99):03d}"

    return (
        brcd,                        # BRCD
        f"C{i:06d}",                 # CUST_NBR
        str(random.randint(1, 9)),   # CUST_NBR_CHKDGT
        f"A{i:06d}",                 # ACCT_NBR
        str(random.randint(1, 9)),   # ACCT_NBR_CHKDGT
        acct_brnch,                  # ACCT_BRNCH
        dob,                         # BRTH_DT
        age,                         # AGE_YRS
        sr_flag,                     # SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG
        kyc,                         # KYC_FLG
        balance,                     # END_OF_DAY_BAL
        _fmt(open_dt),               # ACCT_OPN_DT
        mtrty_dt,                    # MTRTY_DT
        tenor_yr,                    # tenor_yr
        tenor_day,                   # tenor_day
        mat_dt,                      # MAT_DT
        new_rate,                    # NEW_EFFCTV_RT
        prdct_id,                    # PRDCT_ID
        pf_fac,                      # PF_FAC_REPT
        product_name,                # PRODUCT_NAME
        branch_name,                 # BRANCHNAME
        circle_name,                 # CIRCLENAME
        circle_cd,                   # CIRCLECD
        exp_rate,                    # EXPECTED_EFFCTV_RT
        is_mod,                      # ISMOD
    )


def generate_rows(count: int, start_i: int) -> list[tuple]:  # type: ignore[type-arg]
    """
    Generate *count* synthetic row tuples starting from index *start_i*.

    ``start_i`` is used for ``CUST_NBR`` / ``ACCT_NBR`` sequential IDs so
    appending rows never collides with existing ones.

    Args:
        count:   Number of rows to generate.
        start_i: Index of the first new row (1-based).

    Returns:
        List of row tuples ready for ``executemany``.
    """
    return [_make_row(start_i + i) for i in range(count)]


def insert_rows(conn: sqlite3.Connection, rows: list[tuple]) -> None:  # type: ignore[type-arg]
    """
    Bulk-insert *rows* into ``COMP_TBL`` using a single ``executemany``.

    Args:
        conn: Open SQLite connection (must already have COMP_TBL).
        rows: Row tuples as returned by :func:`generate_rows`.
    """
    conn.executemany(_INSERT_SQL, rows)
    conn.commit()
    print(f"[insert] {len(rows):,} rows inserted into COMP_TBL.")


def print_summary(conn: sqlite3.Connection, db_path: Path, inserted: int) -> None:
    """
    Print a statistics summary after the insert.

    Args:
        conn:     Open SQLite connection.
        db_path:  Path to the database file (for display only).
        inserted: Number of rows that were just inserted.
    """
    total: int   = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM COMP_TBL").fetchone()[0]
    sr: int      = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM COMP_TBL WHERE SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG = 1").fetchone()[0]
    kyc_n: int   = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM COMP_TBL WHERE KYC_FLG = 'N'").fetchone()[0]
    circles: list[tuple[str, int]] = conn.execute(
        "SELECT CIRCLENAME, COUNT(*) FROM COMP_TBL GROUP BY CIRCLENAME ORDER BY 2 DESC LIMIT 5"
    ).fetchall()

    print(f"\n{'='*55}")
    print(f"  Database : {db_path}")
    print(f"  Inserted : {inserted:,} rows")
    print(f"  Total    : {total:,} rows in COMP_TBL")
    print(f"{'='*55}")
    print(f"  Senior citizens  (SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG=1) : {sr:,}")
    print(f"  KYC non-compliant (KYC_FLG='N')        : {kyc_n:,}")
    print("\n  Top 5 circles by row count:")
    for name, cnt in circles:
        print(f"    {name:<22} {cnt:>5}")
    print(f"{'='*55}\n")


def seed(db_path: Path, count: int, reset: bool) -> None:
    """
    Full end-to-end pipeline:

    1. :func:`ensure_database` — create ``.db`` file and parent dirs if absent.
    2. :func:`ensure_table`    — create ``COMP_TBL`` if absent or stale.
    3. :func:`reset_table`     — (only when ``reset=True``) delete all rows.
    4. :func:`generate_rows`   — build synthetic row tuples in memory.
    5. :func:`insert_rows`     — bulk-insert into the database.
    6. :func:`print_summary`   — display post-insert statistics.

    Args:
        db_path: Path to the SQLite ``.db`` file.
        count:   Number of rows to insert (0 = create DB/table only).
        reset:   When ``True``, delete all existing rows before inserting.
    """
    ensure_database(db_path)

    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(db_path))

    try:
        ensure_table(conn)

        if reset:
            reset_table(conn)

        if count == 0:
            print("[info]   --count 0: skipping row insert.")
            print_summary(conn, db_path, inserted=0)
            return

        start_i: int = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM COMP_TBL").fetchone()[0] + 1
        rows = generate_rows(count, start_i)

        insert_rows(conn, rows)

        print_summary(conn, db_path, inserted=count)

    finally:
        conn.close()


def _parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description=(
            "Create the SQLite database + COMP_TBL schema and insert synthetic data.\n"
            "Safe to re-run: creates only what is missing."
        ),
        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
    )
    p.add_argument(
        "--count", type=int, default=2000,
        help="Number of rows to insert (default: 2000). Use 0 to create DB/table only.",
    )
    p.add_argument(
        "--reset", action="store_true",
        help="Delete all existing rows before inserting.",
    )
    p.add_argument(
        "--db", type=Path,
        default=Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "comp_db" / "COMP_REPORTS.db",
        help="Path to the SQLite database file.",
    )
    p.add_argument(
        "--seed", type=int, default=42,
        help="Random seed for reproducibility (default: 42).",
    )
    return p.parse_args()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    args = _parse_args()
    random.seed(args.seed)
    seed(db_path=args.db, count=args.count, reset=args.reset)
