# Adding a New SQLite Database

This page walks you through connecting a second (or third) SQLite database to the pipeline with **minimal code changes** — the architecture is designed so that adding a database is almost entirely a configuration task.

**Total files to edit: 2** (plus your `.db` file itself).

---

## Overview

The pipeline discovers databases at runtime by:

1. `SchemaChecker` scanning the `data_root` folder for `*.db` files and scoring each one against the question.
2. `SQLWriterAgent` reading table metadata (descriptions, column definitions, few-shots) from `genai/config/databases.toml`.

You do **not** need to touch any Python files.

---

## Step 1 — Place the `.db` file

Put the SQLite database file anywhere under `data/`:

```
data/
├── comp_db/
│   └── COMP_REPORTS.db          ← existing
└── my_new_db/
    └── MY_NEW_DATABASE.db       ← new file
```

> The exact sub-folder name does not matter — `SchemaChecker` uses `data_root.glob("**/*.db")`.

If you need to create the database from a CSV or schema script, run that first and place the `.db` file here.

---

## Step 2 — Register in `databases.toml`

Open `genai/config/databases.toml` and add a new `[databases.<key>]` block.

```toml
# ── existing database (leave untouched) ────────────────────────
[databases.COMP_REPORTS]
db_file     = "data/comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db"
description = "Banking compliance reports database for term-deposit products."

[databases.COMP_REPORTS.tables.COMP_TBL]
description  = "..."
filter_query = "SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL WHERE ACCT_BRNCH IN ({branch_code})"

[databases.COMP_REPORTS.tables.COMP_TBL.columns]
BRCD = "BRANCH CODE OR BRANCH NUMBER"
# ... other columns ...

[databases.COMP_REPORTS.tables.COMP_TBL.few_shots]
examples = """
Question: ...
Response:
<begin_sql_response>
SELECT ...
<end_sql_response>
"""


# ── NEW database ────────────────────────────────────────────────
[databases.MY_NEW_DATABASE]
db_file     = "data/my_new_db/MY_NEW_DATABASE.db"
description = "Short description of what this database contains."

[databases.MY_NEW_DATABASE.tables.MY_TABLE]
description  = "What this table represents."
filter_query = "SELECT * FROM MY_TABLE WHERE BRANCH_ID = '{branch_id}'"

[databases.MY_NEW_DATABASE.tables.MY_TABLE.columns]
BRANCH_ID    = "Unique identifier for the branch"
CUSTOMER_ID  = "Unique customer identifier"
PRODUCT_TYPE = "Type of financial product"
BALANCE      = "Current account balance in INR"
STATUS       = "Account status: ACTIVE, CLOSED, DORMANT"

[databases.MY_NEW_DATABASE.tables.MY_TABLE.few_shots]
examples = """
Question: Show all active accounts in MY_TABLE.
Response:
<begin_sql_response>
SELECT * FROM MY_TABLE WHERE STATUS = 'ACTIVE';
<end_sql_response>

Question: Count accounts by product type.
Response:
<begin_sql_response>
SELECT PRODUCT_TYPE, COUNT(*) AS cnt FROM MY_TABLE GROUP BY PRODUCT_TYPE ORDER BY cnt DESC;
<end_sql_response>
"""
```

### `databases.toml` field reference

| Field | Required | Description |
|-------|----------|-------------|
| `db_file` | ✓ | Relative path to the `.db` file from the project root |
| `description` | recommended | Injected into the LLM prompt as context |
| `tables.<NAME>.description` | recommended | Per-table description in the LLM prompt |
| `tables.<NAME>.filter_query` | optional | Template for branch-filtered queries |
| `tables.<NAME>.columns.<COL>` | recommended | Business description of each column |
| `tables.<NAME>.few_shots.examples` | strongly recommended | NL→SQL examples for this table |

---

## Step 3 — Update `.env` (only if making it the default)

By default the backend uses the database configured under `DATABASE__DB_FOLDER` / `DATABASE__DB_NAME` in `.env`. If you want **this** new database to be the default (used when `SchemaChecker` scores are tied), update:

```ini
# .env
DATABASE__DB_FOLDER=data/my_new_db
DATABASE__DB_NAME=MY_NEW_DATABASE.db
```

If you want to keep `COMP_REPORTS.db` as the default but also support the new one, **no `.env` change is needed** — `SchemaChecker` will automatically route questions to the best-matching database based on keyword overlap.

---

## Step 4 — Verify

```powershell
# From project root
python -c "
from utils.registry import _build_db_registry
reg = _build_db_registry()
print(list(reg.keys()))
"
```

You should see `MY_TABLE` (or whatever your table name is) in the output alongside `COMP_TBL`.

---

## Step 5 — Test with a question

Start the server and ask a question specific to the new table:

```bash
curl -s -X POST http://127.0.0.1:8080/api/v1/compliance/orchestrate \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"question": "Show all active accounts in MY_TABLE"}' | python -m json.tool
```

Check `stages` in the response — you should see `schema_checker:db=MY_NEW_DATABASE.db`.

---

## Troubleshooting

| Symptom | Likely cause | Fix |
|---------|-------------|-----|
| SchemaChecker still uses old DB | Keyword scores tied | Add more specific question keywords in `few_shots.examples` |
| `MY_TABLE` not in registry | Typo in `databases.toml` table key | Key must exactly match the SQLite table name |
| `FileNotFoundError` in registry | Wrong `db_file` path | Path is relative to project root, not to `genai/config/` |
| SQL always wrong for new table | No few-shots | Add at least 3 diverse `few_shots.examples` |
| Column names rejected by reviewer | Table not in `sqlite_master` | Ensure the `.db` file exists and the table was created |

---

## Minimal Change Summary

To add a new SQLite database you need to:

| # | File | What to add |
|---|------|-------------|
| 1 | `data/<folder>/<NAME>.db` | The SQLite database file itself |
| 2 | `genai/config/databases.toml` | One `[databases.<KEY>]` block per database, one `[tables.<NAME>]` block per table |

That's it. No Python changes required.

---

## Advanced: Making a Table the Default for All Queries

If your new table should be the **only** table the pipeline ever queries (i.e., you want to replace `COMP_TBL`):

1. Set `DATABASE__DB_NAME` in `.env` to your new `.db` file.
2. Set `COMPLIANCE_TABLE_NAME` in `utils/config.py`:

```python
COMPLIANCE_TABLE_NAME: str = "MY_TABLE"   # was "COMP_TBL"
```

3. Update `SQLWriterAgent` default:

```python
# genai/nl2sql_agent/sql_writer_agent.py
class SQLWriterAgent:
    def __init__(
        self,
        model_service: ModelLoaderService,
        table_name: str = "MY_TABLE",    # was COMPLIANCE_TABLE_NAME
    ) -> None:
```



####################################################################################################
FILE: docs\agents.md
####################################################################################################

# Agent Nodes

The NL-to-SQL pipeline is a six-node **LangGraph StateGraph**.  
Every node receives the full `AgentState` dict, reads the keys it needs, and returns a **partial** dict containing only the keys it owns.

---

## Shared State — `AgentState`

`genai/nl2sql_agent/state.py`

```python
class AgentState(TypedDict, total=False):
    # ── Input ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    question:             str          # raw user question
    question_id:          str | None   # optional idempotency key

    # ── Node 1 outputs ─────────────────────────────────────
    interpreted_question: str          # normalised question with trailing ?
    intent_ok:            bool         # True = proceed, False = clarify
    clarification_needed: str | None   # message shown to the user when False

    # ── Node 2 outputs ─────────────────────────────────────
    db_path:              str          # absolute path to the chosen .db file
    schema_context:       str          # Markdown schema block injected into LLM prompt

    # ── Node 3 outputs ─────────────────────────────────────
    sql_query:            str          # raw SQL returned by the LLM

    # ── Node 4 outputs ─────────────────────────────────────
    sql_review_passed:    bool
    review_feedback:      str | None   # sent back to Node 3 on retry
    retry_count:          int

    # ── Node 4.5 outputs ───────────────────────────────────
    sqlglot_valid:        bool
    sqlglot_error:        str | None

    # ── Node 5 outputs ─────────────────────────────────────
    result_rows:          list[dict[str, object]]
    result_columns:       list[str]
    result_summary:       str

    # ── Shared bookkeeping ─────────────────────────────────
    error:                str | None
    stages:               list[str]    # append-only audit trail (LangGraph reducer)
```

The `stages` field uses `Annotated[list[str], operator.add]` so LangGraph **appends** each node's entries rather than overwriting the whole list.

---

## Node 1 — IntentChecker

**File:** `genai/nl2sql_agent/intent_checker.py`  
**Prompt config:** `genai/prompt/intent_checker.yaml`  
**Type:** Rule-based (no LLM call)

### What it does

Runs four sequential checks. The first check that fails returns `intent_ok=False` immediately; all four must pass for the pipeline to continue.

| Check | Rule | Failure example |
|-------|------|----------------|
| **Empty** | question is blank after strip | `""` or `"   "` |
| **Disallowed action** | token matches DROP / DELETE / INSERT / UPDATE / TRUNCATE / ALTER / CREATE / REPLACE / PRAGMA | `"delete all accounts"` |
| **Too short** | fewer than 3 meaningful tokens **and** no metric term | `"pending"` |
| **Vague / ambiguous** | phrase is in `_VAGUE_PHRASES` set, or contains an ambiguous pronoun with no metric term | `"give me data"`, `"please verify this"` |

### Metric term bypass

If the question contains any of the 24 metric terms (`age`, `kyc`, `balance`, `maturity`, `account`, `count`, `sum`, `top` …) the too-short check is skipped, allowing short but specific questions like `"show KYC"`.

### Outputs written

```
interpreted_question   — stripped + trailing "?" added
intent_ok              — True / False
clarification_needed   — None or YAML message string
stages                 — ["intent_checker:ok"] or ["intent_checker:too_short"] etc.
```

### Routing after Node 1

```
intent_ok=True  → Node 2 (SchemaChecker)
intent_ok=False → clarify_node → END
```

---

## Node 2 — SchemaChecker

**File:** `genai/nl2sql_agent/schema_checker.py`  
**Prompt config:** `genai/prompt/schema_checker.yaml`  
**Type:** Rule-based (no LLM call)

### What it does

1. Scans `data_root` (the DB folder) for every `*.db` file.
2. Scores each file by keyword overlap between the question and table/column names.
3. Selects the highest-scoring `.db` file.
4. Builds a **Markdown schema context** string containing:
   - Table names with column types (from `PRAGMA table_info`)
   - Sample rows (up to `schema.sample_row_limit` from `schema_checker.yaml`, default 3)
   - Column business descriptions from `databases.toml`

### Scoring algorithm

```
score = 1  if db_filename_stem is in the question (case-insensitive)
     += 2  for each table name found in the question
     += 1  for each column name found in the question
```

### Outputs written

```
db_path        — absolute path to the winning .db file
schema_context — Markdown string (injected verbatim into the LLM prompt)
stages         — ["schema_checker:db=COMP_REPORTS.db"]
```

---

## Node 3 — SQLWriterAgent

**File:** `genai/nl2sql_agent/sql_writer_agent.py`  
**Prompt config:** `genai/prompt/sql_writer.yaml`  
**Type:** **LLM call** (only node that calls the LLM)

### What it does

1. `ContextBuilder.build()` assembles the full prompt from:
   - System role (from `sql_writer.yaml`)
   - Numbered rules list
   - Table description + column block (from `databases.toml`)
   - Live schema context (from Node 2)
   - Few-shot examples (from `databases.toml`)
   - Reviewer feedback (present only on retries)
2. Calls `ModelLoaderService.generate(prompt)`.
3. Extracts SQL from between `<begin_sql_response>` … `<end_sql_response>` tags.

### Retry behaviour

On retry (coming back from Node 4 or 4.5), `review_feedback` is non-null and gets injected as a `[REVIEWER FEEDBACK]` block at the bottom of the prompt, so the LLM can see exactly what was wrong.

### Outputs written

```
sql_query    — extracted SQL string (or "" if extraction failed)
retry_count  — unchanged (passed through)
stages       — ["sql_writer_agent:llm_generated retry=0"]
error        — set only if no SQL tags found in output
```

---

## Node 4 — SQLReviewer

**File:** `genai/nl2sql_agent/sql_reviewer.py`  
**Prompt config:** `genai/prompt/sql_review.yaml`  
**Type:** Rule-based (no LLM call)

### What it does

Four checks in order:

| # | Check | Action on fail |
|---|-------|---------------|
| 1 | `retry_count >= max_retries` (default 3) | Sets `error`, routes to Executor to surface the message |
| 2 | SQL is empty | Increments retry, routes back to Writer |
| 3 | Contains disallowed DML/DDL keyword | Increments retry, routes back to Writer |
| 4 | Does not start with `SELECT` | Increments retry, routes back to Writer |
| 5 | Missing `FROM` clause | Increments retry, routes back to Writer |
| 6 | Referenced tables don't exist in the `.db` file | Increments retry, routes back to Writer |

### Routing after Node 4

```
error set        → Executor (surfaces the error message to user)
passed=True      → Node 4.5 (SQLValidator)
passed=False     → Node 3 (SQLWriter, retry)
```

---

## Node 4.5 — SQLValidator

**File:** `genai/nl2sql_agent/sql_validator.py`  
**Type:** Rule-based — uses **sqlglot** AST parser (no LLM call)

### What it does

Three checks:

1. **Parse validity** — `sqlglot.parse()` must succeed without raising `ParseError`.
2. **Statement type** — parsed AST root must be a `Select` node.
3. **Dialect canonicalisation** — rewrites the SQL to canonical SQLite syntax via `stmt.sql(dialect="sqlite")`.

If sqlglot is not installed the node passes transparently (`sqlglot_valid=True`).

### Routing after Node 4.5

```
valid   → Executor
invalid → Node 3 (SQLWriter, retry with sqlglot error in feedback)
```

---

## Node 5 — ExecutorAgent

**File:** `genai/nl2sql_agent/executor_agent.py`  
**Config:** `genai/config/query.toml`  
**Type:** Rule-based (no LLM call)

### What it does

1. Applies the row-limit policy from `query.toml`:
   - If `enforce_default_limit=true` and the SQL has no `LIMIT` clause, appends `LIMIT <default_limit>`.
2. Opens a `BEGIN DEFERRED` transaction.
3. Runs `cursor.fetchmany(max_display_rows)` — hard ceiling regardless of SQL.
4. **Always rolls back** (unconditional in `finally`).
5. Builds the result summary string.

### Read-only guarantee

```python
conn.execute("BEGIN DEFERRED")   # deferred = no write locks acquired
# ... execute SELECT ...
conn.rollback()                  # always, even on success
```

Even if a malicious SQL somehow bypassed all prior guards, the rollback ensures no data is ever written.

### Outputs written

```
result_rows     — list of {column: value} dicts
result_columns  — ordered list of column names
result_summary  — one-sentence human-readable summary
error           — None on success, error message on failure
stages          — ["executor_agent:ok rows=42"]
```

---

## Adding a New Node

1. Create `genai/nl2sql_agent/my_node.py` with a class that has `.run(state: AgentState) -> AgentState`.
2. Add a `my_node.yaml` under `genai/prompt/` (optional, only if it has user-facing messages).
3. In `genai/nl2sql_agent/graph.py`:
   - Import your node class.
   - Add `graph.add_node("my_node", MyNode().run)`.
   - Wire it with `graph.add_edge(...)` or `graph.add_conditional_edges(...)`.
4. Write tests in `tests/test_my_node.py`.



####################################################################################################
FILE: docs\api.md
####################################################################################################

# REST API Reference

**Base URL:** `http://127.0.0.1:<PORT>`  (default PORT = 8080 from `.env`)  
**Interactive docs:** `http://127.0.0.1:<PORT>/docs` (Swagger UI)  
**ReDoc:** `http://127.0.0.1:<PORT>/redoc`

---

## `GET /health`

Liveness probe. Returns immediately without touching the LLM or database.

**Response 200**
```json
{
  "status": "ok",
  "version": "2.0.0"
}
```

**curl**
```bash
curl http://127.0.0.1:8080/health
```

---

## `POST /api/v1/config/llm`

Reconfigure the LLM provider at runtime and test the connection. On success, the running LangGraph pipeline is replaced with a new one using the updated settings.

**Request body**
```json
{
  "provider":       "dlh",
  "model_name":     "google/gemma-4-31b-it",
  "dlh_base_url":   "http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1",
  "dlh_api_key":    "EMPTY",
  "dlh_verify_ssl": false
}
```

All fields are optional — omitted fields keep the current `.env` values.

**Response 200**
```json
{
  "success":  true,
  "provider": "dlh",
  "message":  "LLM connection successful. LangGraph pipeline is ready."
}
```

**Response 500** (connection failed)
```json
{
  "error": "Provider API error: ..."
}
```

**curl**
```bash
curl -s -X POST http://127.0.0.1:8080/api/v1/config/llm \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"model_name": "google/gemma-4-31b-it"}' | python -m json.tool
```

---

## `POST /api/v1/compliance/chat`

Simplified NL-to-SQL query. Returns the SQL and result rows without agent-level detail.

**Request body**
```json
{
  "question":    "How many senior citizen accounts have KYC flag N?",
  "question_id": "req-001"
}
```

`question_id` is optional — a UUID is generated server-side if omitted.

**Response 200**
```json
{
  "question_id":    "req-001",
  "sql_query":      "SELECT COUNT(*) AS cnt FROM COMP_TBL WHERE SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG = 1 AND KYC_FLG = 'N'",
  "df_output":      [{"cnt": 142}],
  "result_columns": ["cnt"],
  "result_summary": "Result: **cnt** = `142`",
  "error":          null
}
```

**Response 200** (intent rejected — `error` contains the clarification message)
```json
{
  "question_id":    "...",
  "sql_query":      null,
  "df_output":      null,
  "result_columns": [],
  "result_summary": null,
  "error":          "Your question is too short or unclear to process."
}
```

**Response 503** (backend not initialised yet)
```json
{
  "detail": "Compliance processor is not initialised. Please retry in a few seconds."
}
```

**curl**
```bash
curl -s -X POST http://127.0.0.1:8080/api/v1/compliance/chat \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"question": "Show accounts with KYC flag N in Mumbai"}' | python -m json.tool
```

---

## `POST /api/v1/compliance/orchestrate`

Full agentic response. Same as `/chat` but includes the complete agent stage audit trail, interpreted question, and all intermediate agent outputs.

**Request body** — same as `/chat`.

**Response 200**
```json
{
  "question_id":            "req-002",
  "interpreted_question":   "Show accounts with KYC flag N in Mumbai?",
  "needs_clarification":    false,
  "clarification_question": null,
  "sql_query":              "SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL WHERE KYC_FLG = 'N' AND UPPER(CIRCLENAME) LIKE '%MUMBAI%'",
  "df_output": [
    {"BRCD": "001", "CUST_NBR": "C000001", "KYC_FLG": "N", "CIRCLENAME": "MUMBAI METRO"}
  ],
  "result_columns": ["BRCD", "CUST_NBR", "KYC_FLG", "CIRCLENAME"],
  "result_summary": "Query returned **1 record(s)**. Columns: BRCD, CUST_NBR, KYC_FLG, CIRCLENAME.",
  "stages": [
    "intent_checker:ok",
    "schema_checker:db=COMP_REPORTS.db",
    "sql_writer_agent:llm_generated retry=0",
    "sql_reviewer:passed",
    "sql_validator:passed dialect=sqlite",
    "executor_agent:ok rows=1"
  ],
  "error": null
}
```

**Response 200** (clarification needed)
```json
{
  "question_id":            "...",
  "needs_clarification":    true,
  "clarification_question": "Your question is unclear. Please rephrase with more detail.",
  "sql_query":              null,
  "df_output":              null,
  "stages":                 ["intent_checker:clarification_required", "clarify_node:terminal"],
  "error":                  null
}
```

---

## Error Codes

| HTTP | When |
|------|------|
| 200 | Success (even if `needs_clarification=true` or `error` is set — pipeline ran to completion) |
| 422 | Pydantic validation error (blank `question` string, wrong types) |
| 503 | Backend not yet initialised (model still loading) |
| 500 | Unhandled domain exception (`ComplianceBotError` subclass) |

---

## Python Client Example

```python
import requests

BASE = "http://127.0.0.1:8080"

def ask(question: str) -> dict:
    r = requests.post(
        f"{BASE}/api/v1/compliance/orchestrate",
        json={"question": question},
        timeout=60,
    )
    r.raise_for_status()
    return r.json()

result = ask("How many senior citizens are in the Delhi circle?")
print(result["sql_query"])
print(result["result_summary"])
for stage in result["stages"]:
    print(" ·", stage)
```

# Architecture

This document contains **seven diagrams** covering different views of the system.

| # | Diagram | What it shows |
|---|---------|---------------|
| 1 | [Folder Tree](#1-folder-tree) | Every file/folder with one-line annotations |
| 2 | [OS Process Diagram](#2-os-process-diagram) | Processes, ports, IPC at the operating-system level |
| 3 | [High-Level Component Layers](#3-high-level-component-layers) | Logical tiers: UI → API → Pipeline → Storage |
| 4 | [LangGraph Pipeline Flow](#4-langgraph-pipeline-flow) | Node-by-node state-graph with all routing edges |
| 5 | [Request Lifecycle Sequence](#5-request-lifecycle-sequence) | End-to-end sequence from browser click to rendered table |
| 6 | [AgentState Data-Flow](#6-agentstate-data-flow) | Which fields each node reads and writes |
| 7 | [Config Resolution Order](#7-config-resolution-order) | How `.env` overrides TOML, and TOML overrides code defaults |

---

## 1. Folder Tree

```
COMPLIANCE-CHATBOT-DLH-V2/                 ← project root (always run from here)
│
├── application/                           ← entry-points & UI
│   ├── main.py                            FastAPI app — backend REST API
│   ├── run_ui.py                          Self-contained HTTP UI server
│   ├── Launcher.py                        Spawns backend + UI together
│   ├── notebook.ipynb                     Jupyter walkthrough
│   └── fonts/                             Urbanist TTF files embedded in run_ui.py
│
├── data/
│   ├── comp_db/
│   │   └── COMP_REPORTS.db                Primary SQLite DB (seed script creates this)
│   ├── logs/
│   │   └── compliance_bot.log             Rotating log — always written here (10 MB × 10)
│   └── seed_synthetic_data.py             CLI — creates + seeds COMP_REPORTS.db
│
├── genai/
│   ├── config/                            Static TOML — no secrets ever here
│   │   ├── application.toml               App display name, server defaults
│   │   ├── database.toml                  DB type, folder, filename
│   │   ├── databases.toml          ★★★    Table registry: columns + few-shots per table
│   │   ├── llm.toml                       Provider, model name, temperature defaults
│   │   ├── logging.toml                   Python logging dictConfig (handlers, levels)
│   │   ├── query.toml                     Row-limit policy (enforce_default_limit)
│   │   └── ui.toml                        Backend URL hint for the UI
│   │
│   ├── core/
│   │   ├── exceptions.py                  Exception hierarchy (ComplianceBotError base)
│   │   ├── model.py                       ModelLoaderService + ProviderManager
│   │   ├── processor.py                   ComplianceProcessor — graph orchestration
│   │   └── schemas.py                     Pydantic API models + factory helpers
│   │
│   ├── nl2sql_agent/
│   │   ├── state.py                       AgentState TypedDict — shared pipeline state
│   │   ├── graph.py                ★★★    Builds & compiles the LangGraph StateGraph
│   │   ├── intent_checker.py              Node 1 — rule-based intent classification
│   │   ├── schema_checker.py              Node 2 — DB selection + Markdown schema
│   │   ├── sql_writer_agent.py            Node 3 — LLM call + SQL extraction
│   │   ├── sql_reviewer.py                Node 4 — regex + table-existence checks
│   │   ├── sql_validator.py               Node 4.5 — sqlglot AST parse + dialect
│   │   ├── executor_agent.py              Node 5 — read-only SQLite + formatting
│   │   └── main.py                        CLI runner (no FastAPI, useful for debugging)
│   │
│   └── prompt/                            YAML prompt files — edit to change LLM wording
│       ├── intent_checker.yaml            User-facing messages for Node 1
│       ├── schema_checker.yaml            Headers + error messages for Node 2
│       ├── sql_writer.yaml                System role, rules, prompt template for Node 3
│       ├── sql_review.yaml                Feedback strings for Node 4
│       ├── common.yaml                    Shared greeting / error boilerplate
│       ├── compliance.yaml                Compliance-domain context strings
│       ├── report_generation.yaml         Report formatting strings
│       └── system.yaml                    Top-level system persona
│
├── utils/                                 Shared — importable by any module
│   ├── config.py                          Pydantic BaseSettings (AppSettings, ModelSettings…)
│   ├── helpers.py                         Stateless stdlib helpers (JSON, I/O, truncate…)
│   ├── logger.py                          get_logger() factory — reads logging.toml
│   ├── registry.py                        DB registry + SQLite helpers + YAML loader
│   └── __init__.py                        Re-exports everything above
│
├── tests/
│   ├── conftest.py                        Shared fixtures: tmp_db, tmp_prompt_dir
│   └── test_*.py                          One file per source module (198 tests)
│
├── docs/                                  ← documentation (this folder)
├── .env                                   Secrets + local overrides — never committed
├── pyproject.toml                         ruff, pytest, mypy configuration
└── requirements.txt                       Python dependencies
```

## 2. OS Process Diagram

What actually runs on the operating system when you start the stack.

```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  Developer Machine / CML Node                                                │
│                                                                              │
│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │
│  │  python application/Launcher.py   (PID: parent)                        │ │
│  │                                                                         │ │
│  │   subprocess.Popen() × 2                                                │ │
│  │           │                         │                                   │ │
│  │           ▼                         ▼                                   │ │
│  │  ┌─────────────────────┐  ┌────────────────────────┐                   │ │
│  │  │ uvicorn             │  │ python run_ui.py        │                   │ │
│  │  │ application.main:app│  │ (http.server)           │                   │ │
│  │  │                     │  │                         │                   │ │
│  │  │  LISTEN: 0.0.0.0    │  │  LISTEN: 0.0.0.0        │                   │ │
│  │  │  PORT:  8001        │  │  PORT:  8100            │                   │ │
│  │  └──────────┬──────────┘  └──────────┬─────────────┘                   │ │
│  │             │  IPC via HTTP           │                                 │ │
│  │             │  CB_BACKEND_URL         │                                 │ │
│  │             └────────────────────────┘                                  │ │
│  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ │
│                                           ▲                                  │
│                                           │  Browser HTTP                    │
│                                           │  http://localhost:8100           │
│  ┌────────────────────────────────────────┴──────────────────────────────┐  │
│  │  Browser tab                                                          │  │
│  │  • Fetches UI HTML/CSS/JS from run_ui.py:8100                        │  │
│  │  • POSTs API calls to backend:8001 (proxied by run_ui.py)            │  │
│  └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘  │
│                                                                              │
│  ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐  │
│  │  File system                                                           │  │
│  │  data/comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db   (SQLite — opened per-request)         │  │
│  │  data/logs/compliance_bot.log   (RotatingFileHandler — always here)   │  │
│  └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘  │
│                                                                              │
│  ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐  │
│  │  Network — outbound only                                               │  │
│  │  uvicorn process  ──HTTPS──►  DLH inference server                    │  │
│  │                              http://<DLH_HOST>:9000/api/v1            │  │
│  │                              (OpenAI-compatible endpoint)              │  │
│  └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘  │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

  Port map (Launcher defaults)
  ────────────────────────────
  8001   FastAPI backend   (change via BACKEND_PORT in Launcher.py)
  8100   UI server         (change via CDSW_APP_PORT env var)
  9000   DLH LLM server    (external — set MODEL__DLH_BASE_URL in .env)
```

## 3. High-Level Component Layers

```
╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
║  PRESENTATION LAYER                                              ║

║   • Maps final AgentState → Pydantic response model             ║
╠══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╣
║  PIPELINE LAYER  (LangGraph StateGraph)                          ║
║                                                                  ║
║   Node 1  IntentChecker    rule-based, no LLM                    ║
║   Node 2  SchemaChecker    rule-based, no LLM                    ║
║   Node 3  SQLWriterAgent   ★ only LLM call in the pipeline       ║
║   Node 4  SQLReviewer      rule-based, no LLM                    ║
║   Node 4.5 SQLValidator    sqlglot AST, no LLM                   ║
║   Node 5  ExecutorAgent    rule-based, no LLM                    ║
╠══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╣
║  INFRASTRUCTURE LAYER                                            ║
║                                                                  ║
║   utils/                                                         ║
║   • config.py      Pydantic Settings — reads .env                ║
║   • logger.py      get_logger() — reads logging.toml             ║
║   • registry.py    YAML loader + SQLite helpers + DB registry    ║
║   • helpers.py     stateless stdlib helpers                      ║
║                                                                  ║
║   genai/config/*.toml   TOML config files (no secrets)           ║
║   genai/prompt/*.yaml   YAML prompt files (LLM wording)          ║
╠══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╣
║  STORAGE LAYER                                                   ║
║                                                                  ║
║   data/comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db   SQLite — read-only at runtime   ║
║   data/logs/compliance_bot.log   Rotating log file               ║
║   .env                           Secrets (never committed)        ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝
```

## 4. LangGraph Pipeline Flow

Full node graph with every conditional edge.

```
                        ┌─────────┐
                        │  START  │
                        └────┬────┘
                             │  AgentState{question}
                             ▼
                  ┌──────────────────────┐
                  │   1. IntentChecker   │  rule-based (4 checks)
                  │   intent_checker.py  │  no LLM call
                  └──────────┬───────────┘
                             │
              ┌──────────────┴──────────────┐
              │ intent_ok=True              │ intent_ok=False
              ▼                             ▼
 ┌────────────────────────┐      ┌──────────────────────┐
 │   2. SchemaChecker     │      │    clarify_node       │
 │   schema_checker.py    │      │  (terminal — returns  │
 │   Scans *.db files,    │      │   clarification msg)  │
 │   scores by keyword    │      └──────────┬────────────┘
 │   overlap, builds      │                 │
 │   Markdown schema      │                 ▼
 └──────────┬─────────────┘             ┌───────┐
            │                           │  END  │
            ▼                           └───────┘
 ┌────────────────────────┐  ◄──────────────────────────┐
 │   3. SQLWriterAgent    │                             │
 │   sql_writer_agent.py  │  retry (with feedback       │
 │   ★ ONLY LLM CALL ★    │  injected into prompt)      │
 │   Builds full prompt + │                             │
 │   calls DLH model +    │                             │
 │   extracts SQL         │                             │
 └──────────┬─────────────┘                             │
            │ sql_query                                 │
            ▼                                           │
 ┌────────────────────────┐                             │
 │   4. SQLReviewer       │  rule-based (6 checks)      │
 │   sql_reviewer.py      │  no LLM call                │
 └──────────┬─────────────┘                             │
            │                                           │
  ┌─────────┼──────────────────┐                        │
  │         │                  │                        │
  │ error   │ passed=True      │ passed=False            │
  │ (max    │                  │ retry_count += 1       │
  │ retries)│                  └────────────────────────┘
  │         ▼                                           ▲
  │  ┌────────────────────────┐                         │
  │  │  4.5 SQLValidator      │  uses sqlglot AST       │
  │  │  sql_validator.py      │  no LLM call            │
  │  └──────────┬─────────────┘                         │
  │             │                                       │
  │     ┌───────┴───────┐                               │
  │     │ valid         │ invalid                       │
  │     │               │ retry_count += 1              │
  │     │               └───────────────────────────────┘
  │     ▼
  │  ┌────────────────────────┐
  └─►│   5. ExecutorAgent     │  BEGIN DEFERRED
     │   executor_agent.py    │  fetchmany(max_display_rows)
     │   Read-only SQLite,    │  unconditional ROLLBACK
     │   format rows +        │
     │   build summary        │
     └──────────┬─────────────┘
                │ result_rows, result_summary, stages
                ▼
            ┌───────┐
            │  END  │
            └───────┘
```

---

## 5. Request Lifecycle Sequence

From browser click to rendered table.

```
Browser          run_ui.py         main.py           LangGraph         DLH LLM     SQLite
   │                │  (proxy)        │  (FastAPI)       │  (graph)        │           │
   │                │                │                  │                 │           │
   │── POST ───────►│                │                  │                 │           │
   │  /api/v1/      │                │                  │                 │           │
   │  compliance/   │── forward ────►│                  │                 │           │
   │  orchestrate   │                │                  │                 │           │
   │                │                │── validate ─────►│                 │           │
   │                │                │  Pydantic        │                 │           │
   │                │                │                  │                 │           │
   │                │                │── invoke ────────►                 │           │
   │                │                │  AgentState       │                │           │
   │                │                │                  │                 │           │
   │                │                │                Node 1 IntentCheck  │           │
   │                │                │                (rule-based)        │           │
   │                │                │                  │                 │           │
   │                │                │                Node 2 SchemaCheck  │           │
   │                │                │                ───────────────────────────────►│
   │                │                │                PRAGMA table_info   │           │
   │                │                │                ◄───────────────────────────────│
   │                │                │                  │                 │           │
   │                │                │                Node 3 SQLWriter    │           │
   │                │                │                ──── LLM call ─────►│           │
   │                │                │                ◄─── SQL text ──────│           │
   │                │                │                  │                 │           │
   │                │                │                Node 4 SQLReviewer  │           │
   │                │                │                (rule-based)        │           │
   │                │                │                  │                 │           │
   │                │                │                Node 4.5 Validator  │           │
   │                │                │                (sqlglot)           │           │
   │                │                │                  │                 │           │
   │                │                │                Node 5 Executor     │           │
   │                │                │                ───── SELECT ──────────────────►│
   │                │                │                ◄──── rows ─────────────────────│
   │                │                │                  │                 │           │
   │                │                │◄── AgentState ───│                 │           │
   │                │                │                  │                 │           │
   │                │                │── map ──────────►│                 │           │
   │                │                │  OrchestrationResponse             │           │
   │                │◄── JSON ───────│                  │                 │           │
   │◄── JSON ───────│                │                  │                 │           │
   │                │                │                  │                 │           │
   │ render table   │                │                  │                 │           │
   │ show SQL       │                │                  │                 │           │
   │ show stages    │                │                  │                 │           │
```

**Typical latency breakdown:**

| Step | Time |
|------|------|
| IntentChecker (rule-based) | < 1 ms |
| SchemaChecker (PRAGMA queries) | 5–20 ms |
| SQLWriterAgent (LLM call) | 1–5 s |
| SQLReviewer (rule-based) | < 1 ms |
| SQLValidator (sqlglot) | < 5 ms |
| ExecutorAgent (SQLite SELECT) | 5–50 ms |
| **Total** | **~2–6 s** |

---

## 6. AgentState Data-Flow

Which fields each node **reads** (→ in) and **writes** (← out).

```
                        AgentState keys
                        ───────────────
question ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────►
question_id ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────►

              Node 1: IntentChecker
              ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
              │ reads:  question                                   │
              │ writes: interpreted_question                       │
              │         intent_ok                                  │
              │         clarification_needed                       │
              │         stages += ["intent_checker:ok"]            │
              └────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

              Node 2: SchemaChecker
              ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
              │ reads:  interpreted_question (or question)          │
              │ writes: db_path                                     │
              │         schema_context                              │
              │         stages += ["schema_checker:db=…"]          │
              └────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

              Node 3: SQLWriterAgent
              ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
              │ reads:  interpreted_question                        │
              │         schema_context                              │
              │         review_feedback   (None on first pass)      │
              │         retry_count                                 │
              │ writes: sql_query                                   │
              │         retry_count       (unchanged, passed thru)  │
              │         stages += ["sql_writer_agent:llm_generated"]│
              └────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

              Node 4: SQLReviewer
              ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
              │ reads:  sql_query                                   │
              │         db_path                                     │
              │         retry_count                                 │
              │ writes: sql_review_passed                           │
              │         review_feedback                             │
              │         retry_count  (incremented on fail)          │
              │         error        (only if max retries hit)      │
              │         stages += ["sql_reviewer:passed"]           │
              └────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

              Node 4.5: SQLValidator
              ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
              │ reads:  sql_query                                   │
              │         retry_count                                 │
              │ writes: sqlglot_valid                               │
              │         sqlglot_error                               │
              │         sql_query   (canonicalised SQLite dialect)  │
              │         retry_count (incremented on fail)           │
              │         stages += ["sql_validator:passed"]          │
              └────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

              Node 5: ExecutorAgent
              ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
              │ reads:  sql_query                                   │
              │         db_path                                     │
              │ writes: result_rows                                 │
              │         result_columns                              │
              │         result_summary                              │
              │         error   (None on success)                   │
              │         stages += ["executor_agent:ok rows=N"]      │
              └────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

result_rows ◄────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
result_columns ◄─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
result_summary ◄─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
stages ◄─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
error ◄──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
```

---

## 7. Config Resolution Order

Settings are layered — later sources win.

```
Priority   Source                     Mechanism
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  LOW   genai/config/*.toml       Default values in TOML files.
        ─────────────────────     Loaded by utils/registry.py and
                                  utils/logger.py at import time.
                                  Change for deployment-wide defaults.

          ▼ overridden by

        utils/config.py           Python-level defaults in Pydantic
        (Field default=…)         Field() declarations.
                                  Only change if TOML doesn't cover it.

          ▼ overridden by

        .env                      Local/deployment secrets and
        ─────────────────────     environment-specific values.
                                  Loaded by pydantic-settings.
                                  Never committed to git.

          ▼ overridden by

  HIGH  Environment variables     Injected by CI, Docker, CML.
        (process environment)     e.g. MODEL__DLH_API_KEY=abc123
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  Nested key syntax (pydantic-settings env_nested_delimiter="__"):
  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │  Python class           .env key                            │
  │  ─────────────          ─────────────────────────────────── │
  │  AppSettings.port       PORT                                │
  │  AppSettings.log_level  LOG_LEVEL                           │
  │  database.db_folder     DATABASE__DB_FOLDER                 │
  │  database.db_name       DATABASE__DB_NAME                   │
  │  model.model_name       MODEL__MODEL_NAME                   │
  │  model.dlh_base_url     MODEL__DLH_BASE_URL                 │
  │  model.dlh_api_key      MODEL__DLH_API_KEY                  │
  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

  Prompt YAML resolution:
  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │                                                             │
  │  utils/registry.py                                         │
  │  _PROMPTS_DIR = <project_root>/genai/prompt/               │
  │         │                                                   │
  │         ├── intent_checker.yaml   ──► _prompt_cache[stem]  │
  │         ├── schema_checker.yaml   ──► _prompt_cache[stem]  │
  │         ├── sql_writer.yaml       ──► _prompt_cache[stem]  │
  │         └── sql_review.yaml       ──► _prompt_cache[stem]  │
  │                                                             │
  │  get_prompt("intent_checker.yaml", "messages", "too_short") │
  │  → walks nested dict → returns string or default           │
  │                                                             │
  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

## Key Design Decisions

| Decision | Rationale |
|----------|-----------|
| All config in TOML + `.env`, zero hard-coded strings | Environment-specific values never touch source code |
| YAML prompts loaded via `utils/registry.py` | Change LLM wording without touching Python; hot-reload via `reload()` |
| `total=False` TypedDict for AgentState | Each node writes only its own keys; LangGraph merges correctly |
| sqlglot AST validation **after** regex review | Defence in depth — catches syntax errors the regex misses |
| `BEGIN DEFERRED` + unconditional rollback in executor | SQLite stays read-only regardless of what SQL reaches the executor |
| `str \| Path` throughout file helpers | Callers can pass either without extra wrapping |
| Logs always anchored to `<project_root>/data/logs/` | Consistent location regardless of which directory the server is started from |

####################################################################################################

# Configuration Reference

Configuration is layered. Later sources override earlier ones:

```
genai/config/*.toml  (lowest priority — hardcoded defaults)
        ↓
      .env           (local overrides — never committed to git)
        ↓
 Environment vars    (highest priority — CI / deployment injection)
```

---

## `.env` — Secrets and Local Overrides

`.env` is loaded by `pydantic-settings` (`AppSettings` in `utils/config.py`).  
It is **ignored by git** — never commit it.

```ini
# Application
APPLICATION_NAME="COMPLIANCE-BOT (UI + LLM Hybrid)"
PAGE_CONFIG="SBI COMPLIANCE-BOT"
LOG_LEVEL=INFO          # DEBUG | INFO | WARNING | ERROR
LOG_DIR=data/logs       # always resolved relative to project root
HOST=0.0.0.0
PORT=8080
DEBUG=false             # true enables uvicorn --reload

# Database (nested via __ delimiter)
DATABASE__DB_TYPE=SQLite
DATABASE__DB_FOLDER=data/comp_db
DATABASE__DB_NAME=COMP_REPORTS.db

# LLM (nested via __ delimiter)
MODEL__PROVIDER=dlh
MODEL__MODEL_NAME=google/gemma-4-31b-it
MODEL__MODEL_MAX_NEW_TOKENS=512
MODEL__MODEL_TEMPERATURE=0.1
MODEL__DLH_BASE_URL=http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1
MODEL__DLH_API_KEY=EMPTY         # replace with real key for auth
MODEL__DLH_VERIFY_SSL=false
```

---

## `genai/config/application.toml`

Human-readable display names and server defaults.

```toml
[application]
name        = "COMPLIANCE-BOT (UI + LLM Hybrid)"
page_config = "SBI COMPLIANCE-BOT"
version     = "2.0.0"

[server]
host      = "0.0.0.0"
port      = 8000
debug     = false
log_level = "INFO"
log_dir   = "data/logs"
```

> These values are **read by** `AppSettings` as defaults; `.env` overrides them at runtime.

---

## `genai/config/database.toml`

```toml
[database]
db_type   = "SQLite"
db_folder = "data/comp_db"
db_name   = "COMP_REPORTS.db"
```

`DatabaseSettings.db_path` resolves these to an absolute path:

```python
Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / self.db_folder / self.db_name
```

---

## `genai/config/databases.toml` ★ Most important config file

The **table registry** — consumed by `utils/registry.py` and injected into every LLM prompt.

```toml
[databases.<KEY>]
db_file     = "data/<folder>/<FILE>.db"   # relative to project root
description = "One sentence about this database."

[databases.<KEY>.tables.<TABLE_NAME>]
description  = "What this table represents."
filter_query = "SELECT * FROM <TABLE_NAME> WHERE BRANCH = '{branch}'"

[databases.<KEY>.tables.<TABLE_NAME>.columns]
COL1 = "Business description of column 1"
COL2 = "Business description of column 2"

[databases.<KEY>.tables.<TABLE_NAME>.few_shots]
examples = """
Question: Natural language question.
Response:
<begin_sql_response>
SELECT ...;
<end_sql_response>
"""
```

See [add-database.md](add-database.md) for the full walkthrough.

---

## `genai/config/llm.toml`

```toml
[llm]
provider             = "dlh"
model_name           = "google/gemma-4-31b-it"
model_max_new_tokens = 512
model_temperature    = 0.1

[llm.dlh]
base_url   = "http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1"
api_key    = "EMPTY"
verify_ssl = false
```

> These are read as fallback defaults. Override via `.env` (`MODEL__*` keys) or via the `/api/v1/config/llm` endpoint at runtime.

---

## `genai/config/query.toml`

Controls how many rows the ExecutorAgent fetches.

```toml
[query]
enforce_default_limit = false
# true  → if SQL has no LIMIT clause, append "LIMIT <default_limit>" before execution
# false → execute SQL exactly as written (default)

default_limit     = 10
# rows appended when enforce_default_limit = true

max_display_rows  = 50
# hard ceiling via fetchmany() — applies regardless of enforce_default_limit
```

**When to use `enforce_default_limit = true`:** if your users frequently forget to add `LIMIT` and end up getting all 50 000 rows.

---

## `genai/config/logging.toml`

See [logging.md](logging.md) for the full reference.

Key fields:

| Key | Default | What to change |
|-----|---------|---------------|
| `handlers.console.level` | `INFO` | Set `DEBUG` to see all agent decisions in terminal |
| `handlers.rotating_file.maxBytes` | `10485760` (10 MB) | Increase if log files fill up too fast |
| `handlers.rotating_file.backupCount` | `10` | How many rolled files to keep |

---

## `genai/config/ui.toml`

```toml
[ui]
backend_url = "http://0.0.0.0:8066"
```

Informational only — `run_ui.py` reads `CB_BACKEND_URL` from the environment (set by `Launcher.py` or manually), not from this file.

---

## `utils/config.py` — Pydantic Settings Classes

```python
class DatabaseSettings(BaseSettings):
    db_type:   str  = "SQLite"
    db_folder: str  = "data/comp_db"
    db_name:   str  = "COMP_REPORTS.db"

    @property
    def db_path(self) -> Path:
        return Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / self.db_folder / self.db_name


class ModelSettings(BaseSettings):
    provider:              str   = "dlh"
    model_name:            str   = "google/gemma-4-31b-it"
    model_max_new_tokens:  int   = 512
    model_temperature:     float = 0.1
    dlh_base_url:          str   = "http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1"
    dlh_api_key:           str   = "EMPTY"
    dlh_verify_ssl:        bool  = False


class AppSettings(BaseSettings):
    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(
        env_file=".env",
        env_nested_delimiter="__",   # DATABASE__DB_NAME → database.db_name
    )
    application_name: str  = "COMPLIANCE-BOT (UI + LLM Hybrid)"
    log_level:        str  = "INFO"
    log_dir:          str  = "data/logs"
    host:             str  = "0.0.0.0"
    port:             int  = 8000
    debug:            bool = False
    database: DatabaseSettings = Field(default_factory=DatabaseSettings)
    model:    ModelSettings    = Field(default_factory=ModelSettings)
```

---

## Constants in `utils/config.py`

```python
SQL_BEGIN_TAG  = "<begin_sql_response>"
SQL_END_TAG    = "<end_sql_response>"

COMPLIANCE_TABLE_NAME     = "COMP_TBL"     # default table for SQLWriterAgent
BRANCH_CODE_PLACEHOLDER   = "{branch_code}"

DEFAULT_MAX_NEW_TOKENS    = 512
DEFAULT_TEMPERATURE       = 0.1
DEFAULT_STOP_SEQUENCES    = ["<end_sql_response>"]
```

Change `COMPLIANCE_TABLE_NAME` if your primary table has a different name.

# Future Enhancements

Planned and proposed improvements to the Compliance Chatbot pipeline.  
Each item describes the **current limitation**, the **proposed change**, and the **files that would need to change**.

---

## Table of Contents

| # | Enhancement | Complexity |
|---|-------------|------------|
| 1 | [Multi-Source Table Registry (Postgres, MySQL, Oracle)](#1-multi-source-table-registry) | Medium |
| 2 | [Vector DB for Table Metadata & Schema Search](#2-vector-db-for-table-metadata--schema-search) | High |
| 3 | [Multi-Agent Parallel Execution](#3-multi-agent-parallel-execution) | High |
| 4 | [Streaming Responses (Server-Sent Events)](#4-streaming-responses) | Medium |
| 5 | [Report Generation Agent](#5-report-generation-agent) | Medium |
| 6 | [Query Cache (Semantic Deduplication)](#6-query-cache-semantic-deduplication) | Medium |
| 7 | [Role-Based Access Control (RBAC)](#7-role-based-access-control) | Medium |

| 10 | [LLM Provider Abstraction (OpenAI / Anthropic / Local)](#10-llm-provider-abstraction) | Low |
| 11 | [Observability — LangSmith / OpenTelemetry Tracing](#11-observability) | Low |
| 12 | [Scheduled Compliance Reports](#12-scheduled-compliance-reports) | Medium |

## 1. Multi-Source Table Registry

### Current limitation

`databases.toml` only registers SQLite `.db` files.  
`SchemaChecker` only scans for `*.db` files on disk.  
`ExecutorAgent` only connects to SQLite via `sqlite3`.

### Proposed design

```
                 ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
                 │           DataSourceRegistry                      │
                 │  (replaces databases.toml + registry.py)          │
                 └──────────┬───────────────────────────────────────┘
                            │
          ┌─────────────────┼──────────────────────┐
          ▼                 ▼                       ▼
  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐       ┌──────────────┐
  │  SQLite      │  │  PostgreSQL  │       │  Oracle /    │
  │  Connector   │  │  Connector   │  ...  │  MySQL       │
  │  (existing)  │  │  (new)       │       │  Connector   │
  └──────────────┘  └──────────────┘       └──────────────┘
```

**Connector interface** (`genai/connectors/base.py`):
```python
class BaseConnector(ABC):
    @abstractmethod
    def execute(self, sql: str) -> tuple[list[str], list[dict]]: ...

    @abstractmethod
    def get_tables(self) -> list[str]: ...

    @abstractmethod
    def get_columns(self, table: str) -> list[tuple]: ...

    @abstractmethod
    def get_sample_rows(self, table: str, limit: int) -> list[tuple]: ...
```

**databases.toml extension**:
```toml
[databases.COMPLIANCE_PG]
type       = "postgresql"
host       = "10.0.0.1"
port       = 5432
dbname     = "compliance"
user_env   = "PG_USER"       # read from environment
password_env = "PG_PASSWORD" # read from environment

[databases.COMPLIANCE_PG.tables.COMP_TBL]
description = "..."
```

**Files to change:**

| File | Change |
|------|--------|
| `genai/connectors/base.py` | New — abstract `BaseConnector` |
| `genai/connectors/sqlite_connector.py` | Extract existing SQLite logic |
| `genai/connectors/postgres_connector.py` | New — psycopg2 / asyncpg |
| `genai/connectors/mysql_connector.py` | New — mysql-connector-python |
| `utils/registry.py` | Replace `fetch_all` / `open_readonly` with connector dispatch |
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/executor_agent.py` | Use connector instead of sqlite3 directly |
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/schema_checker.py` | Use connector for PRAGMA / information_schema |
| `genai/config/databases.toml` | Add `type` field per database |

---

## 2. Vector DB for Table Metadata & Schema Search

### Current limitation

`SchemaChecker` uses simple keyword overlap scoring to pick the right database.  
For 2–3 databases this works fine, but at 20+ databases it breaks down — keywords like `account` appear everywhere.

### Proposed design

Replace keyword scoring with **semantic similarity** using a vector DB.

```
Boot time (one-off):
──────────────────
  databases.toml
       │
       ▼ embed each table's description + column names
  ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │  EmbeddingService                              │
  │  (e.g. sentence-transformers / DLH embed API) │
  └────────────────┬───────────────────────────────┘
                   │ vectors
                   ▼
  ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │  Vector Store                                  │
  │  • ChromaDB  (local, zero-infra)               │
  │  • Qdrant    (self-hosted)                     │
  │  • Pinecone  (managed)                         │
  │  • Weaviate  (self-hosted)                     │
  └────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

Per-request (SchemaChecker Node 2):
────────────────────────────────────
  user question
       │
       ▼ embed
  EmbeddingService
       │ query vector
       ▼
  Vector Store  ──► top-K similar table records
       │
       ▼
  ranked (db_name, table_name, score)
       │
       ▼
  Build schema context for top-1 (or merge top-K)
```

**What gets stored per table:**

```python
{
  "id":          "COMP_REPORTS::COMP_TBL",
  "text":        "Compliance table for term-deposit products targeting senior-citizen customers. "
                 "Columns: BRCD (branch code), CUST_NBR (customer number), KYC_FLG ...",
  "metadata": {
      "db_stem":    "COMP_REPORTS",
      "db_file":    "data/comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db",
      "table_name": "COMP_TBL",
  }
}
```

**Files to change / add:**

| File | Change |
|------|--------|
| `genai/embeddings/embedding_service.py` | New — wraps sentence-transformers or DLH embed |
| `genai/embeddings/vector_store.py` | New — ChromaDB / Qdrant abstraction |
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/schema_checker.py` | Replace `_score()` with vector similarity search |
| `utils/registry.py` | Add `build_vector_index()` called once at startup |
| `application/main.py` | Call `build_vector_index()` in `lifespan()` |
| `requirements.txt` | Add `chromadb`, `sentence-transformers` or `qdrant-client` |

---

## 3. Multi-Agent Parallel Execution

### Current limitation

The pipeline is strictly sequential — one node runs at a time.  
`SchemaChecker` and `IntentChecker` could run in parallel.  
For multi-database queries, multiple `ExecutorAgent` instances could run simultaneously.

### Proposed design

```
Current (sequential):
  IntentChecker → SchemaChecker → SQLWriter → Reviewer → Validator → Executor

Proposed (parallel where safe):

  ┌─────────────────┐
  │  IntentChecker  │
  └────────┬────────┘
           │
    ┌──────┴───────┐
    │              │
    ▼              ▼
  Schema        Pre-fetch        ← parallel fan-out via LangGraph Send API
  Checker       few-shots
    │              │
    └──────┬───────┘
           │
         SQLWriter
           │
    ┌──────┴───────┐
    │              │
    ▼              ▼
  Reviewer   Validator           ← run these in parallel (both read sql_query)
    │              │
    └──────┬───────┘
           │
        Executor

Multi-DB fan-out:
  SQLWriter generates SQL for each database in parallel
  Executor runs each in parallel, results merged by a FanInAgent
```

**LangGraph `Send` API** (v0.2+):

```python
from langgraph.types import Send

def fan_out_to_executors(state: AgentState) -> list[Send]:
    return [
        Send("executor_agent", {**state, "db_path": db})
        for db in state["candidate_dbs"]
    ]

graph.add_conditional_edges("sql_writer_agent", fan_out_to_executors)
graph.add_node("fan_in_agent", merge_results)
```

**Files to change:**

| File | Change |
|------|--------|
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/graph.py` | Add `Send`-based fan-out edges |
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/state.py` | Add `candidate_dbs: list[str]`, `all_results: list[...]` |
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/fan_in_agent.py` | New — merges results from parallel executors |
| `requirements.txt` | Ensure `langgraph>=0.2` (already present) |

---

## 4. Streaming Responses

### Current limitation

`POST /api/v1/compliance/orchestrate` returns only when the entire pipeline is done.  
Users see nothing for 2–6 seconds.

### Proposed design

```
Client                    FastAPI                 LangGraph
  │                          │                       │
  │── POST /orchestrate ─────►│                       │
  │                          │── graph.astream() ───►│
  │                          │                       │ Node 1 done
  │◄── data: stage=intent ───│◄── AgentState chunk ──│
  │                          │                       │ Node 2 done
  │◄── data: stage=schema ───│◄── AgentState chunk ──│
  │                          │                       │ Node 3 (LLM stream)
  │◄── data: token=SELECT ───│◄── token stream ──────│
  │◄── data: token=* ────────│                        │
  │◄── data: token=FROM ─────│                        │
  │                          │                       │ Node 5 done
  │◄── data: rows=[...] ─────│◄── final state ───────│
```

**New endpoint** (`application/main.py`):

```python
from fastapi.responses import StreamingResponse

@app.post("/api/v1/compliance/stream")
async def compliance_stream(request: ComplianceChatRequest):
    async def event_generator():
        async for chunk in graph.astream(initial_state):
            yield f"data: {chunk.model_dump_json()}\n\n"
    return StreamingResponse(event_generator(), media_type="text/event-stream")
```

**Files to change:**

| File | Change |
|------|--------|
| `application/main.py` | Add `GET /api/v1/compliance/stream` SSE endpoint |
| `genai/core/processor.py` | Add `stream_query_with_orchestration()` async generator |
| `application/run_ui.py` | Update chat JS to consume SSE and render tokens progressively |

---

## 5. Report Generation Agent

### Current limitation

Results are returned as raw rows + a one-line summary.  
There is no agent that formats results into a business compliance report.

### Proposed design

Add a **Node 6 — ReportGenerationAgent** after the Executor.

```
  ExecutorAgent
       │ result_rows, result_columns, result_summary
       ▼
  ┌────────────────────────────────┐
  │   6. ReportGenerationAgent     │
  │   report_generation.yaml       │
  │                                │
  │   • Calls LLM with rows +      │
  │     summary as context         │
  │   • Produces a structured      │
  │     Markdown report:           │
  │       - Executive summary      │
  │       - Key metrics            │
  │       - Highlighted anomalies  │
  │       - Recommendations        │
  │   • Optionally exports PDF     │
  └────────────────┬───────────────┘
                   │ report_markdown, report_pdf_path
                   ▼
                 END
```

**New state fields:**
```python
report_markdown: str          # Markdown report string
report_pdf_path: str | None   # Path to generated PDF (optional)
```

**Files to add / change:**

| File | Change |
|------|--------|
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/report_agent.py` | New — LLM-based report generator |
| `genai/prompt/report_generation.yaml` | Already exists — populate with report template |
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/state.py` | Add `report_markdown`, `report_pdf_path` |
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/graph.py` | Add Node 6, wire after Executor |
| `genai/core/schemas.py` | Add `report_markdown` to `ComplianceOrchestrationResponse` |
| `application/run_ui.py` | Render report Markdown in a new tab |

---

## 6. Query Cache (Semantic Deduplication)

### Current limitation

Every question hits the LLM, even if a semantically identical question was asked 10 seconds ago.

### Proposed design

```
Incoming question
      │
      ▼ embed + cosine similarity
  ┌──────────────────────────────────┐
  │  SemanticCache                   │
  │  (Redis or in-memory dict)       │
  │                                  │
  │  key:   embedding vector         │
  │  value: AgentState (final)       │
  │  TTL:   5 minutes (configurable) │
  └──────────┬───────────────────────┘
             │
    ┌────────┴────────┐
    │ cache HIT       │ cache MISS
    ▼                 ▼
  return cached    run full pipeline
  AgentState       → cache result
```

**Cache location:** A new `Node 0 — CacheChecker` before `IntentChecker`, and a new `Node 6 — CacheWriter` after `ExecutorAgent`.

**Files to add / change:**

| File | Change |
|------|--------|
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/cache_agent.py` | New — check + write semantic cache |
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/graph.py` | Wire CacheChecker before Node 1, CacheWriter after Node 5 |
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/state.py` | Add `cache_hit: bool` |
| `utils/config.py` | Add `CacheSettings` (TTL, backend: memory/redis) |
| `requirements.txt` | Add `redis` (optional, only if Redis backend chosen) |

---

## 7. Role-Based Access Control (RBAC)

### Current limitation

There is no authentication or authorisation — any caller can query any table.

### Proposed design

```
Request
  │
  ├── Bearer token (JWT)
  │
  ▼
┌───────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  FastAPI dependency: verify_token()       │
│  Decodes JWT → {user_id, roles, branches} │
└────────────────────┬──────────────────────┘
                     │
          ┌──────────┴──────────────┐
          │ role=ANALYST            │ role=AUDITOR
          ▼                         ▼
   Can query own         Can query all branches
   branch only           (no filter injected)
          │
          ▼
  RowFilterAgent (new node between Schema + Writer)
  • Injects WHERE ACCT_BRNCH IN ({user_branches})
    into the generated SQL automatically
```

**Files to add / change:**

| File | Change |
|------|--------|
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/row_filter_agent.py` | New — injects branch filter into SQL |
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/state.py` | Add `user_id`, `user_roles`, `allowed_branches` |
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/graph.py` | Add RowFilterAgent after SQLValidator |
| `application/main.py` | Add `Depends(verify_token)` to compliance endpoints |
| `utils/config.py` | Add `AuthSettings` (JWT secret, algorithm) |
| `requirements.txt` | Add `python-jose[cryptography]`, `passlib` |

---

## 8. Multi-Turn Conversation Memory

### Current limitation

Each request is completely stateless — the pipeline has no memory of previous questions in the same session.

### Proposed design

```
Session store (Redis / in-memory):
  session_id → [
    {"role": "user",      "content": "Show KYC N accounts"},
    {"role": "assistant", "content": "SQL: SELECT..., rows: [...]"},
    {"role": "user",      "content": "Now filter by Mumbai only"},
    ...
  ]

HistoryAgent (new Node 0.5):
  • Reads last N turns from session store
  • Appends conversation history to the LLM prompt
  • Resolves pronouns ("filter that result") using prior context
```

**New request field:**
```python
class ComplianceChatRequest(BaseModel):
    question:   str | None = None
    session_id: str | None = None   # NEW — ties requests to a conversation
```

**Files to add / change:**

| File | Change |
|------|--------|
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/history_agent.py` | New — session retrieval + context injection |
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/state.py` | Add `session_id`, `conversation_history` |
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/graph.py` | Add HistoryAgent between IntentChecker and SchemaChecker |
| `genai/core/schemas.py` | Add `session_id` to `ComplianceChatRequest` |
| `utils/config.py` | Add `SessionSettings` (backend, TTL, max_turns) |

---

## 9. Auto Schema Discovery

### Current limitation

Adding a new database requires **manually** writing the `[databases.<KEY>]` block in `databases.toml`.  
Column descriptions and few-shot examples must be written by hand.

### Proposed design

A one-shot CLI tool that inspects a `.db` file and auto-generates the TOML block:

```powershell
python -m genai.tools.discover_schema \
    --db data/new_db/MY_DATABASE.db \
    --output genai/config/databases.toml \
    --append
```

```
.db file
   │
   ├── PRAGMA table_info → column names + types
   ├── SELECT * LIMIT 5  → sample data
   └── sqlite_master     → table descriptions (if comments stored)
   │
   ▼
LLM call (optional):
  "Given this table schema and sample rows, write:
   1. A one-sentence table description.
   2. A business description for each column.
   3. Three diverse NL→SQL few-shot examples."
   │
   ▼
Generated TOML block appended to databases.toml
```

**Files to add:**

| File | Change |
|------|--------|
| `genai/tools/discover_schema.py` | New — CLI tool |
| `genai/tools/__init__.py` | New |

---

## 10. LLM Provider Abstraction

### Current limitation

Only the DLH on-prem inference server (OpenAI-compatible) is supported.  
Switching to OpenAI, Anthropic, or a local Ollama instance requires editing Python code.

### Proposed design

The `ProviderManager` class in `genai/core/model.py` already exists as a registry.  
Extending it:

```python
# .env
MODEL__PROVIDER=openai          # dlh | openai | anthropic | ollama

# New providers auto-registered at startup:
ProviderManager.register("openai",    OpenAIConnector(settings))
ProviderManager.register("anthropic", AnthropicConnector(settings))
ProviderManager.register("ollama",    OllamaConnector(settings))
ProviderManager.register("dlh",       DLHConnector(settings))   # existing
```

All connectors implement the same `.generate(prompt) -> str` interface.

**Files to change:**

| File | Change |
|------|--------|
| `genai/core/model.py` | Extract `DLHConnector`; add `OpenAIConnector`, `AnthropicConnector`, `OllamaConnector` |
| `utils/config.py` | Add `provider` routing in `ModelSettings` |
| `requirements.txt` | Add `anthropic` (optional) |

---

## 11. Observability

### Current limitation

Logging is the only observability mechanism.  
There is no distributed tracing, no latency histograms, no LLM call cost tracking.

### Proposed design

```
LangSmith (LLM-specific tracing):
  • Every LLM call logged with: prompt, response, latency, token count
  • LangGraph node traces visible in LangSmith dashboard
  • Add LANGCHAIN_TRACING_V2=true + LANGCHAIN_API_KEY to .env

OpenTelemetry (general tracing):
  • Span per LangGraph node
  • FastAPI request spans (auto-instrumented)
  • SQLite query spans
  • Export to Jaeger / Tempo / Honeycomb
```

**Files to change:**

| File | Change |
|------|--------|
| `application/main.py` | Add `FastAPIInstrumentor().instrument_app(app)` |
| `genai/nl2sql_agent/graph.py` | Add OTel span context per node |
| `.env` | Add `LANGCHAIN_TRACING_V2`, `LANGCHAIN_API_KEY`, `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT` |
| `requirements.txt` | Add `langsmith`, `opentelemetry-sdk`, `opentelemetry-instrumentation-fastapi` |

---

## 12. Scheduled Compliance Reports

### Current limitation

All queries are interactive (user-triggered).  
There is no mechanism to run scheduled compliance checks and email/export results.

### Proposed design

```
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  APScheduler / Celery Beat                             │
│                                                        │
│  Cron: every day at 06:00                              │
│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────┐  │
│  │  ScheduledReportJob                              │  │
│  │  1. Read report definitions from reports.toml   │  │
│  │  2. For each report:                             │  │
│  │     a. Call compliance pipeline with question   │  │
│  │     b. Generate PDF via ReportGenerationAgent  │  │
│  │     c. Email PDF to configured recipients       │  │
│  │     d. Upload to HDFS / S3 (optional)           │  │
│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────┘  │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

**New config file** `genai/config/reports.toml`:
```toml
[[reports]]
name       = "Daily KYC Non-Compliance"
question   = "Show all accounts where KYC flag is N, grouped by circle"
schedule   = "0 6 * * *"          # cron expression
recipients = ["compliance@sbi.co.in"]
format     = "pdf"

[[reports]]
name       = "Weekly Senior Citizen Summary"
question   = "Count senior citizen accounts by product and circle for this week"
schedule   = "0 8 * * MON"
recipients = ["audit@sbi.co.in"]
format     = "xlsx"
```

**Files to add / change:**

| File | Change |
|------|--------|
| `genai/scheduler/job_runner.py` | New — APScheduler job definitions |
| `genai/scheduler/report_mailer.py` | New — email dispatch (smtplib / sendgrid) |
| `genai/config/reports.toml` | New — scheduled report definitions |
| `application/main.py` | Start scheduler in `lifespan()` |
| `requirements.txt` | Add `apscheduler`, `reportlab` or `weasyprint` |
