"""tests/test_executor_helpers.py — format_summary, to_markdown_table, to_csv, extract_scalar."""

from genai.nl2sql_agent.executor_agent import (
    extract_scalar,
    format_summary,
    to_csv,
    to_markdown_table,
)

# ── format_summary ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class TestFormatSummary:
    def test_no_rows(self):
        msg = format_summary([], [])
        assert "no matching" in msg.lower() or "no" in msg.lower()

    def test_single_aggregate(self):
        rows = [{"COUNT(*)": 42}]
        msg = format_summary(rows, ["COUNT(*)"])
        assert "42" in msg
        assert "COUNT(*)" in msg

    def test_multi_row(self):
        rows = [{"COL": i} for i in range(5)]
        msg = format_summary(rows, ["COL"])
        assert "5" in msg
        assert "COL" in msg

    def test_limit_injected_note(self):
        rows = [{"A": 1}] * 3
        msg = format_summary(rows, ["A"], limit_injected=True, default_limit=3)
        assert "first 3" in msg or "3" in msg

    def test_max_display_rows_note(self):
        rows = [{"A": i} for i in range(10)]
        msg = format_summary(rows, ["A"], limit_injected=False, max_display_rows=10)
        assert "first 10" in msg or "10" in msg


# ── to_markdown_table ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class TestToMarkdownTable:
    def test_empty_rows(self):
        assert to_markdown_table([], ["A"]) == "(no rows)"

    def test_header_present(self):
        rows = [{"A": 1, "B": 2}]
        result = to_markdown_table(rows, ["A", "B"])
        assert "| A | B |" in result

    def test_separator_present(self):
        rows = [{"A": 1}]
        result = to_markdown_table(rows, ["A"])
        assert "---" in result

    def test_data_row_present(self):
        rows = [{"NAME": "Alice"}]
        result = to_markdown_table(rows, ["NAME"])
        assert "Alice" in result

    def test_max_rows_trim(self):
        rows = [{"N": i} for i in range(30)]
        result = to_markdown_table(rows, ["N"], max_rows=5)
        assert "more row(s)" in result

    def test_no_trim_under_limit(self):
        rows = [{"N": i} for i in range(3)]
        result = to_markdown_table(rows, ["N"], max_rows=10)
        assert "more row(s)" not in result


# ── to_csv ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class TestToCsv:
    def test_has_header(self):
        rows = [{"A": 1, "B": 2}]
        csv_str = to_csv(rows, ["A", "B"])
        assert csv_str.startswith("A,B")

    def test_data_row_present(self):
        rows = [{"A": 10, "B": 20}]
        csv_str = to_csv(rows, ["A", "B"])
        assert "10" in csv_str
        assert "20" in csv_str

    def test_empty_rows(self):
        csv_str = to_csv([], ["A"])
        assert "A" in csv_str  # header still written
        lines = [ln for ln in csv_str.splitlines() if ln]
        assert len(lines) == 1  # only header


# ── extract_scalar ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class TestExtractScalar:
    def test_single_cell(self):
        assert extract_scalar([{"COUNT(*)": 42}]) == 42

    def test_multi_row_returns_none(self):
        assert extract_scalar([{"A": 1}, {"A": 2}]) is None

    def test_multi_col_returns_none(self):
        assert extract_scalar([{"A": 1, "B": 2}]) is None

    def test_empty_returns_none(self):
        assert extract_scalar([]) is None

"""tests/test_intent_checker.py — all four intent-check rules (no LLM)."""

import pytest

from genai.nl2sql_agent.intent_checker import IntentChecker
from genai.nl2sql_agent.state import AgentState


@pytest.fixture
def checker():
    return IntentChecker()


class TestEmptyQuestion:
    def test_empty_string(self, checker):
        result = checker.run(AgentState(question=""))
        assert result["intent_ok"] is False
        assert result["clarification_needed"] is not None

    def test_whitespace_only(self, checker):
        result = checker.run(AgentState(question="   "))
        assert result["intent_ok"] is False

    def test_stage_tag(self, checker):
        result = checker.run(AgentState(question=""))
        assert any("empty" in s for s in result["stages"])


class TestDisallowedActionWords:
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("word", [
        "delete", "drop", "insert", "update", "truncate",
        "alter", "create", "replace", "pragma",
    ])
    def test_blocked_word_detected(self, checker, word):
        result = checker.run(AgentState(question=f"Please {word} the table"))
        assert result["intent_ok"] is False
        assert "disallowed_action" in result["stages"][0]

    def test_blocked_word_in_message(self, checker):
        result = checker.run(AgentState(question="delete all accounts"))
        msg = result.get("clarification_needed", "")
        assert "DELETE" in msg or "delete" in msg.lower()

    def test_safe_word_not_blocked(self, checker):
        result = checker.run(AgentState(question="How many senior citizens are there?"))
        assert result["intent_ok"] is True


class TestTooShort:
    def test_single_word(self, checker):
        # "pending" is not a metric term — should be rejected as too short
        result = checker.run(AgentState(question="pending"))
        assert result["intent_ok"] is False

    def test_two_stop_words(self, checker):
        result = checker.run(AgentState(question="what is"))
        assert result["intent_ok"] is False

    def test_metric_term_bypasses_short_check(self, checker):
        # Even a short question with a metric term should pass
        result = checker.run(AgentState(question="show KYC"))
        assert result["intent_ok"] is True


class TestClarification:
    def test_pure_vague_phrase(self, checker):
        result = checker.run(AgentState(question="give me data"))
        assert result["intent_ok"] is False

    def test_ambiguous_with_no_metric(self, checker):
        # "this" is in _AMBIGUOUS_TOKENS; "please verify this" has no metric term
        result = checker.run(AgentState(question="please verify this"))
        assert result["intent_ok"] is False

    def test_specific_compliance_question_passes(self, checker):
        result = checker.run(AgentState(question="How many accounts have KYC flag N?"))
        assert result["intent_ok"] is True

    def test_circle_question_passes(self, checker):
        result = checker.run(AgentState(question="Show all accounts in the Mumbai circle"))
        assert result["intent_ok"] is True


class TestNormalise:
    def test_adds_question_mark(self, checker):
        result = checker.run(AgentState(question="How many accounts have KYC flag N"))
        assert result["interpreted_question"].endswith("?")

    def test_strips_trailing_dot(self, checker):
        result = checker.run(AgentState(question="List all senior citizen accounts."))
        assert result["interpreted_question"].endswith("?")
        assert not result["interpreted_question"].endswith(".?")

"""tests/test_schema_checker.py — SchemaChecker introspection against tmp SQLite."""

from pathlib import Path

from genai.nl2sql_agent.schema_checker import (
    SchemaChecker,
    load_columns,
    load_sample_rows,
    load_table_names,
)
from genai.nl2sql_agent.state import AgentState


class TestLoadTableNames:
    def test_returns_list(self, tmp_db):
        names = load_table_names(tmp_db)
        assert isinstance(names, list)

    def test_comp_tbl_present(self, tmp_db):
        assert "COMP_TBL" in load_table_names(tmp_db)

    def test_missing_file_returns_empty(self):
        assert load_table_names(Path("/nonexistent/db.db")) == []


class TestLoadColumns:
    def test_returns_tuples(self, tmp_db):
        cols = load_columns(tmp_db, "COMP_TBL")
        assert len(cols) > 0

    def test_first_col_is_brcd(self, tmp_db):
        cols = load_columns(tmp_db, "COMP_TBL")
        col_names = [c[1] for c in cols]
        assert "BRCD" in col_names

    def test_missing_table_returns_empty(self, tmp_db):
        assert load_columns(tmp_db, "NO_TABLE") == []

    def test_missing_db_returns_empty(self):
        assert load_columns(Path("/no/db.db"), "COMP_TBL") == []


class TestLoadSampleRows:
    def test_returns_tuple(self, tmp_db):
        col_names, rows = load_sample_rows(tmp_db, "COMP_TBL", limit=2)
        assert isinstance(col_names, list)
        assert isinstance(rows, list)

    def test_limit_respected(self, tmp_db):
        _, rows = load_sample_rows(tmp_db, "COMP_TBL", limit=2)
        assert len(rows) == 2

    def test_column_names_non_empty(self, tmp_db):
        col_names, _ = load_sample_rows(tmp_db, "COMP_TBL", limit=1)
        assert len(col_names) > 0
        assert "BRCD" in col_names

    def test_missing_db_returns_empty(self):
        cols, rows = load_sample_rows(Path("/no/db.db"), "COMP_TBL", limit=2)
        assert cols == []
        assert rows == []


class TestSchemaCheckerRun:
    def test_run_sets_db_path(self, tmp_db):
        checker = SchemaChecker(
            data_root=tmp_db.parent,
            default_db_path=tmp_db,
        )
        state = AgentState(question="Show accounts", interpreted_question="Show accounts?")
        result = checker.run(state)
        assert result.get("db_path") is not None

    def test_schema_context_non_empty(self, tmp_db):
        checker = SchemaChecker(
            data_root=tmp_db.parent,
            default_db_path=tmp_db,
        )
        state = AgentState(question="Show accounts", interpreted_question="Show accounts?")
        result = checker.run(state)
        ctx = result.get("schema_context", "")
        assert len(ctx) > 0

    def test_schema_context_contains_table_name(self, tmp_db):
        checker = SchemaChecker(
            data_root=tmp_db.parent,
            default_db_path=tmp_db,
        )
        state = AgentState(question="Show accounts", interpreted_question="Show accounts?")
        result = checker.run(state)
        assert "COMP_TBL" in result.get("schema_context", "")

    def test_stage_tag_set(self, tmp_db):
        checker = SchemaChecker(
            data_root=tmp_db.parent,
            default_db_path=tmp_db,
        )
        state = AgentState(question="How many KYC N accounts?")
        result = checker.run(state)
        assert any("schema_checker" in s for s in result.get("stages", []))

"""tests/test_schemas.py — Pydantic models and factory helpers."""

import pytest

from genai.core.schemas import (
    ComplianceChatRequest,
    ComplianceChatResponse,
    ComplianceOrchestrationResponse,
    HealthResponse,
    LLMConnectionResponse,
    build_chat_response,
    build_error_response,
    build_health_response,
    build_llm_connection_response,
    build_orchestration_response,
)


class TestComplianceChatRequest:
    def test_question_stored(self):
        r = ComplianceChatRequest(question="How many accounts?")
        assert r.question == "How many accounts?"

    def test_blank_question_raises(self):
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):
            ComplianceChatRequest(question="   ")

    def test_question_stripped(self):
        r = ComplianceChatRequest(question="  hello  ")
        assert r.question == "hello"

    def test_optional_question_id(self):
        r = ComplianceChatRequest(question="Q", question_id="req-001")
        assert r.question_id == "req-001"

    def test_defaults(self):
        r = ComplianceChatRequest()
        assert r.question is None
        assert r.question_id is None


class TestComplianceChatResponse:
    def test_defaults_are_none(self):
        r = ComplianceChatResponse()
        assert r.sql_query is None
        assert r.df_output is None
        assert r.error is None
        assert r.result_columns == []


class TestComplianceOrchestrationResponse:
    def test_defaults(self):
        r = ComplianceOrchestrationResponse()
        assert r.needs_clarification is False
        assert r.stages == []
        assert r.df_output is None


class TestHealthResponse:
    def test_defaults(self):
        r = HealthResponse()
        assert r.status == "ok"
        assert r.version == "2.0.0"


class TestLLMConnectionResponse:
    def test_fields(self):
        r = LLMConnectionResponse(success=True, provider="dlh", message="ok")
        assert r.success is True
        assert r.provider == "dlh"


class TestFactoryHelpers:
    def test_build_health_response(self):
        r = build_health_response()
        assert r.status == "ok"
        assert r.version == "2.0.0"

    def test_build_health_response_custom_version(self):
        r = build_health_response(version="3.0.0")
        assert r.version == "3.0.0"

    def test_build_chat_response(self):
        r = build_chat_response(
            question_id="q1",
            sql_query="SELECT 1",
            df_output=[{"a": 1}],
            result_columns=["a"],
            result_summary="1 row",
            error=None,
        )
        assert r.question_id == "q1"
        assert r.sql_query == "SELECT 1"
        assert r.result_columns == ["a"]
        assert r.error is None

    def test_build_orchestration_response_defaults(self):
        r = build_orchestration_response(question_id="x")
        assert r.question_id == "x"
        assert r.needs_clarification is False
        assert r.stages == []

    def test_build_error_response(self):
        r = build_error_response(question_id="q2", error_message="boom")
        assert r.error == "boom"
        assert "pipeline:error" in r.stages

    def test_build_llm_connection_response(self):
        r = build_llm_connection_response(success=True, provider="dlh", message="Connected")
        assert r.success is True
        assert r.provider == "dlh"

    def test_build_orchestration_response_with_clarification(self):
        r = build_orchestration_response(
            question_id="q",
            needs_clarification=True,
            clarification_question="Please rephrase?",
        )
        assert r.needs_clarification is True
        assert r.clarification_question == "Please rephrase?"

"""tests/test_sql_reviewer.py — regex/structure SQL review (no LLM, no DB)."""

import pytest

from genai.nl2sql_agent.sql_reviewer import RetryHandler, SQLReviewer
from genai.nl2sql_agent.state import AgentState


@pytest.fixture
def reviewer():
    return SQLReviewer(max_retries=3)


# ── RetryHandler ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class TestRetryHandler:
    def test_initial_count(self):
        h = RetryHandler(max_retries=3)
        state = AgentState(retry_count=0)
        assert h.current_count(state) == 0

    def test_should_retry_when_under_max(self):
        h = RetryHandler(max_retries=3)
        assert h.should_retry(AgentState(retry_count=2)) is True

    def test_should_not_retry_at_max(self):
        h = RetryHandler(max_retries=3)
        assert h.should_retry(AgentState(retry_count=3)) is False

    def test_increment(self):
        h = RetryHandler(max_retries=3)
        result = h.increment(AgentState(retry_count=1))
        assert result["retry_count"] == 2


# ── SQLReviewer ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class TestMaxRetries:
    def test_max_retries_exceeded(self, reviewer):
        state = AgentState(sql_query="SELECT 1", retry_count=3)
        result = reviewer.run(state)
        assert result["sql_review_passed"] is False
        assert any("max_retries" in s for s in result["stages"])

    def test_error_message_set(self, reviewer):
        state = AgentState(sql_query="SELECT 1", retry_count=3)
        result = reviewer.run(state)
        assert result.get("error") is not None


class TestEmptySql:
    def test_empty_sql_fails(self, reviewer):
        result = reviewer.run(AgentState(sql_query="", retry_count=0))
        assert result["sql_review_passed"] is False
        assert any("empty_sql" in s for s in result["stages"])


class TestDisallowedKeywords:
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("kw", ["INSERT", "DELETE", "DROP", "UPDATE", "TRUNCATE"])
    def test_disallowed_keyword_fails(self, reviewer, kw):
        result = reviewer.run(AgentState(sql_query=f"{kw} something", retry_count=0))
        assert result["sql_review_passed"] is False
        assert any("disallowed_keyword" in s for s in result["stages"])

    def test_select_passes_keyword_check(self, reviewer):
        result = reviewer.run(AgentState(
            sql_query="SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL", retry_count=0, db_path=""
        ))
        assert result["sql_review_passed"] is True


class TestStructureChecks:
    def test_missing_select_prefix_fails(self, reviewer):
        result = reviewer.run(AgentState(sql_query="FROM COMP_TBL", retry_count=0))
        assert result["sql_review_passed"] is False
        assert any("no_select" in s for s in result["stages"])

    def test_missing_from_fails(self, reviewer):
        result = reviewer.run(AgentState(sql_query="SELECT *", retry_count=0))
        assert result["sql_review_passed"] is False
        assert any("missing_from" in s for s in result["stages"])

    def test_valid_select_passes(self, reviewer):
        result = reviewer.run(AgentState(
            sql_query="SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL", retry_count=0, db_path=""
        ))
        assert result["sql_review_passed"] is True


class TestTableExistence:
    def test_unknown_table_fails(self, reviewer, tmp_db):
        result = reviewer.run(AgentState(
            sql_query="SELECT * FROM NO_SUCH_TABLE",
            retry_count=0,
            db_path=str(tmp_db),
        ))
        assert result["sql_review_passed"] is False
        assert any("unknown_tables" in s for s in result["stages"])

    def test_known_table_passes(self, reviewer, tmp_db):
        result = reviewer.run(AgentState(
            sql_query="SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL",
            retry_count=0,
            db_path=str(tmp_db),
        ))
        assert result["sql_review_passed"] is True

    def test_retry_incremented_on_fail(self, reviewer):
        result = reviewer.run(AgentState(sql_query="", retry_count=1))
        assert result["retry_count"] == 2

"""tests/test_sql_validator.py — AST-level SQL validation (no LLM, no DB)."""

import pytest

from genai.nl2sql_agent.sql_validator import SQLValidator
from genai.nl2sql_agent.state import AgentState


@pytest.fixture
def validator():
    return SQLValidator(dialect="sqlite")


class TestValidSelect:
    def test_simple_select_passes(self, validator):
        state = AgentState(sql_query="SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL WHERE KYC_FLG = 'N'")
        result = validator.run(state)
        assert result["sqlglot_valid"] is True
        assert result.get("sqlglot_error") is None

    def test_select_with_where_passes(self, validator):
        state = AgentState(sql_query="SELECT COUNT(*) FROM COMP_TBL")
        result = validator.run(state)
        assert result["sqlglot_valid"] is True

    def test_select_with_limit_passes(self, validator):
        state = AgentState(sql_query="SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL LIMIT 10")
        result = validator.run(state)
        assert result["sqlglot_valid"] is True

    def test_stage_tag_passed(self, validator):
        state = AgentState(sql_query="SELECT 1")
        result = validator.run(state)
        assert any("passed" in s for s in result["stages"])


class TestDisallowedStatements:
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("sql", [
        "DELETE FROM COMP_TBL",
        "DROP TABLE COMP_TBL",
        "INSERT INTO COMP_TBL VALUES (1)",
        "UPDATE COMP_TBL SET KYC_FLG = 'Y'",
    ])
    def test_dml_fails(self, validator, sql):
        result = validator.run(AgentState(sql_query=sql))
        assert result["sqlglot_valid"] is False
        assert result["sqlglot_error"] is not None


class TestSyntaxErrors:
    def test_garbled_sql_fails(self, validator):
        result = validator.run(AgentState(sql_query="SELCT * FORM table"))
        assert result["sqlglot_valid"] is False

    def test_empty_sql_fails(self, validator):
        result = validator.run(AgentState(sql_query=""))
        assert result["sqlglot_valid"] is False


class TestRetryIncrement:
    def test_retry_incremented_on_fail(self, validator):
        state = AgentState(sql_query="DELETE FROM t", retry_count=1)
        result = validator.run(state)
        assert result["retry_count"] == 2

# ── safe_json_loads ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class TestSafeJsonLoads:
    def test_valid_json_object(self):
        assert safe_json_loads('{"a": 1}') == {"a": 1}

    def test_invalid_json_returns_none(self):
        assert safe_json_loads("not json") is None

    def test_empty_string_returns_none(self):
        assert safe_json_loads("") is None

    def test_none_input_returns_none(self):
        assert safe_json_loads(None) is None  # type: ignore[arg-type]


# ── truncate ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class TestTruncate:
    def test_short_string_unchanged(self):
        assert truncate("hello", max_len=10) == "hello"

    def test_long_string_truncated(self):
        result = truncate("hello world", max_len=7, suffix="…")
        assert len(result) == 7
        assert result.endswith("…")

    def test_exact_length_unchanged(self):
        assert truncate("hello", max_len=5) == "hello"

    def test_custom_suffix(self):
        result = truncate("abcdefgh", max_len=5, suffix="...")
        assert result == "ab..."


# ── normalise_whitespace ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class TestNormaliseWhitespace:
    def test_collapses_spaces(self):
        assert normalise_whitespace("a  b   c") == "a b c"

    def test_collapses_tabs_and_newlines(self):
        assert normalise_whitespace("a\t\nb") == "a b"

    def test_strips_leading_trailing(self):
        assert normalise_whitespace("  hello  ") == "hello"

    def test_empty_string(self):
        assert normalise_whitespace("") == ""


# ── flatten_list ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class TestFlattenList:
    def test_flat_list_unchanged(self):
        assert flatten_list([1, 2, 3]) == [1, 2, 3]

    def test_one_level_nested(self):
        assert flatten_list([[1, 2], [3], 4]) == [1, 2, 3, 4]

    def test_empty_list(self):
        assert flatten_list([]) == []

    def test_mixed_types(self):
        assert flatten_list([[1, "a"], [True]]) == [1, "a", True]

class TestSafeLoadsDumps:
    def test_safe_loads_valid(self):
        assert safe_loads('{"x": 42}') == {"x": 42}

    def test_safe_loads_invalid_returns_none(self):
        assert safe_loads("bad") is None

    def test_safe_dumps_produces_json(self):
        s = safe_dumps({"a": 1})
        assert '"a": 1' in s

    def test_safe_dumps_non_serialisable(self):
        from datetime import date
        result = safe_dumps({"d": date(2025, 1, 1)})
        assert "2025-01-01" in result

        assert result == [1, 2, 3]

        assert extract_json_from_text("no json here") is None

    def test_extract_json_object_returns_dict(self):
        assert extract_json_object('{"k": "v"}') == {"k": "v"}

    def test_extract_json_object_returns_empty_on_array(self):
        assert extract_json_object("[1, 2]") == {}

# ── file helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class TestFileHelpers:
    def test_ensure_directory(self, tmp_path):
        d = tmp_path / "new" / "nested"
        result = ensure_directory(d)
        assert result.is_dir()

    def test_write_and_read_text(self, tmp_path):
        f = tmp_path / "test.txt"
        write_text(f, "hello pytest")
        assert read_text(f) == "hello pytest"

    def test_file_size(self, tmp_path):
        f = tmp_path / "data.txt"
        f.write_bytes(b"1234567890")
        assert file_size(f) == 10

    def test_file_size_nonexistent(self, tmp_path):
        assert file_size(tmp_path / "missing.txt") == 0

    def test_list_files_finds_files(self, tmp_path):
        (tmp_path / "a.txt").write_text("a")
        (tmp_path / "b.txt").write_text("b")
        found = list_files(tmp_path, pattern="*.txt")
        assert len(found) == 2

    def test_list_files_pattern_filter(self, tmp_path):
        (tmp_path / "a.txt").write_text("a")
        (tmp_path / "b.yaml").write_text("b")
        found = list_files(tmp_path, pattern="*.yaml")
        assert len(found) == 1
        assert found[0].suffix == ".yaml"

    def test_returns_path_object(self):
        p = resolve_project_path("data")
        assert isinstance(p, type(p))  # is a Path

    def test_path_is_absolute(self):
        p = resolve_project_path("data", "comp_db")
        assert p.is_absolute()

class TestGetLogger:

        logger = get_logger("compliance_bot.test")
        assert isinstance(logger, logging.Logger)

    def test_name_matches(self):
        logger = get_logger("compliance_bot.specific")
        assert logger.name == "compliance_bot.specific"

    def test_same_name_same_instance(self):
        a = get_logger("compliance_bot.same")
        b = get_logger("compliance_bot.same")
        assert a is b

    def test_different_names_different_instances(self):
        a = get_logger("compliance_bot.aaa")
        b = get_logger("compliance_bot.bbb")
        assert a is not b

    def test_logging_configured_after_first_call(self):
        get_logger("compliance_bot.check_configured")
        # After first call the flag must be True
        from utils import logger as logger_module
        assert logger_module._LOGGING_CONFIGURED is True

"""tests/test_utils_registry.py — unit tests for utils/registry.py prompt helpers."""

import pytest

from utils.registry import get_prompt, load_prompt


class TestLoadPrompt:
    def test_loads_yaml_file(self, tmp_prompt_dir, monkeypatch):
        """load_prompt should read from the tmp prompt dir."""
        import utils.registry as reg
        monkeypatch.setattr(reg, "_PROMPTS_DIR", tmp_prompt_dir)
        monkeypatch.setattr(reg, "_prompt_cache", {})

        data = load_prompt("intent_checker.yaml")
        assert isinstance(data, dict)
        assert "messages" in data

    def test_cache_hit(self, tmp_prompt_dir, monkeypatch):
        """Second load should return cached object."""
        import utils.registry as reg
        monkeypatch.setattr(reg, "_PROMPTS_DIR", tmp_prompt_dir)
        monkeypatch.setattr(reg, "_prompt_cache", {})

        d1 = load_prompt("intent_checker.yaml")
        d2 = load_prompt("intent_checker.yaml")
        assert d1 is d2

    def test_missing_file_raises(self, tmp_prompt_dir, monkeypatch):
        import utils.registry as reg
        monkeypatch.setattr(reg, "_PROMPTS_DIR", tmp_prompt_dir)
        monkeypatch.setattr(reg, "_prompt_cache", {})

        with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError):
            load_prompt("does_not_exist.yaml")

    def test_stem_only_accepted(self, tmp_prompt_dir, monkeypatch):
        """load_prompt should accept bare stem without extension."""
        import utils.registry as reg
        monkeypatch.setattr(reg, "_PROMPTS_DIR", tmp_prompt_dir)
        monkeypatch.setattr(reg, "_prompt_cache", {})

        data = load_prompt("intent_checker")
        assert "messages" in data


class TestGetPrompt:
    def test_nested_key_lookup(self, tmp_prompt_dir, monkeypatch):
        import utils.registry as reg
        monkeypatch.setattr(reg, "_PROMPTS_DIR", tmp_prompt_dir)
        monkeypatch.setattr(reg, "_prompt_cache", {})

        val = get_prompt("intent_checker.yaml", "messages", "empty_question")
        assert val == "No question provided."

    def test_missing_key_returns_default(self, tmp_prompt_dir, monkeypatch):
        import utils.registry as reg
        monkeypatch.setattr(reg, "_PROMPTS_DIR", tmp_prompt_dir)
        monkeypatch.setattr(reg, "_prompt_cache", {})

        val = get_prompt("intent_checker.yaml", "messages", "nonexistent", default="fallback")
        assert val == "fallback"

    def test_missing_key_returns_none_by_default(self, tmp_prompt_dir, monkeypatch):
        import utils.registry as reg
        monkeypatch.setattr(reg, "_PROMPTS_DIR", tmp_prompt_dir)
        monkeypatch.setattr(reg, "_prompt_cache", {})

        val = get_prompt("intent_checker.yaml", "nonexistent_section")
        assert val is None

"""
utils/config.py — application settings and shared constants.

Merges:
  - config_loader.py  (Pydantic BaseSettings — loads from .env)
  - constants.py      (application-wide literal constants)

All secrets (API keys) must be provided via .env or environment variables.
TOML config files under genai/config/ provide default values;
environment variables always take precedence.
"""

from pathlib import Path

from pydantic import Field
from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings, SettingsConfigDict

SQL_BEGIN_TAG: str = "<begin_sql_response>"
SQL_END_TAG: str   = "<end_sql_response>"

COMPLIANCE_TABLE_NAME: str      = "COMP_TBL"
BRANCH_CODE_PLACEHOLDER: str    = "{branch_code}"

RESPONSE_QUESTION_ID: str = "question_id"
RESPONSE_SQL_QUERY: str   = "sql_query"
RESPONSE_DF_OUTPUT: str   = "df_output"

LOGGER_ROOT_NAME: str = "compliance_bot"

DEFAULT_MAX_NEW_TOKENS: int       = 512
DEFAULT_TEMPERATURE: float        = 0.1
DEFAULT_STOP_SEQUENCES: list[str] = [SQL_END_TAG]



class DatabaseSettings(BaseSettings):
    """Database connection configuration."""

    db_type:   str = Field(default="SQLite",        description="Database engine type.")
    db_folder: str = Field(default="data/comp_db",  description="Relative path to the DB folder.")
    db_name:   str = Field(default="COMP_REPORTS.db", description="SQLite database filename.")

    @property
    def db_path(self) -> Path:
        """Resolve absolute path to the SQLite database file."""
        return Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / self.db_folder / self.db_name


class ModelSettings(BaseSettings):
    """LLM configuration for the Compliance Chatbot (DLH on-prem)."""

    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(extra="ignore")

    provider: str = Field(default="dlh", description="LLM provider (dlh).")
    model_name: str = Field(
        default="google/gemma-4-31b-it",
        description="Remote model name for the DLH OpenAI-compatible endpoint.",
    )
    model_max_new_tokens: int   = Field(default=512,  description="Maximum tokens to generate.")
    model_temperature:    float = Field(default=0.1,  description="Sampling temperature.")
    dlh_base_url: str = Field(
        default="http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1",
        description="DLH inference server base URL.",
    )
    dlh_api_key:    str  = Field(default="EMPTY", description="Bearer token for the DLH endpoint.")
    dlh_verify_ssl: bool = Field(default=False,   description="Verify TLS certificates for DLH.")


class AppSettings(BaseSettings):
    """Top-level application configuration."""

    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(
        env_file=".env",
        env_file_encoding="utf-8",
        env_nested_delimiter="__",
        extra="ignore",
        populate_by_name=True,
    )

    application_name: str  = Field(default="COMPLIANCE-BOT (UI + LLM Hybrid)")
    page_config:      str  = Field(default="SBI COMPLIANCE-BOT")
    log_level:        str  = Field(default="INFO")
    log_dir:          str  = Field(default="data/logs")
    host:             str  = Field(default="0.0.0.0")  # noqa: S104
    port:             int  = Field(default=8000)
    debug:            bool = Field(default=False)

    database: DatabaseSettings = Field(default_factory=DatabaseSettings)
    model:    ModelSettings    = Field(default_factory=ModelSettings)


_settings: AppSettings = AppSettings()


def get_settings() -> AppSettings:
    """Return the cached application settings singleton."""
    return _settings



####################################################################################################
FILE: utils\helpers.py
####################################################################################################



"""
utils/helpers.py — stateless utility helpers.

Merges:
  - common.py    (safe_json_loads, truncate, normalise_whitespace, flatten_list)
  - file_utils.py (ensure_directory, read/write text/bytes, safe_delete, list_files …)
  - json_utils.py (safe_loads, safe_dumps, extract_json_from_text, …)

Nothing in this file has side-effects or external dependencies beyond the
Python standard library.
"""

import json
import re
import shutil
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any


def safe_json_loads(text: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """Try to parse text as JSON; return None if parsing fails."""
    try:
        return json.loads(text)
    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
        return None


def truncate(text: str, max_len: int = 300, suffix: str = "…") -> str:
    """Shorten text to max_len characters and append suffix if it was cut."""
    if len(text) <= max_len:
        return text
    return text[: max_len - len(suffix)] + suffix


def normalise_whitespace(text: str) -> str:
    """Collapse any run of whitespace (spaces, tabs, newlines) into a single space."""
    return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text).strip()


def flatten_list(nested: list[Any]) -> list[Any]:
    """
    Flatten exactly one level of nesting in a list.

    Example: [[1, 2], [3], 4] → [1, 2, 3, 4]
    """
    result: list[Any] = []
    for item in nested:
        if isinstance(item, list):
            result.extend(item)
        else:
            result.append(item)
    return result


def ensure_directory(path: str | Path) -> Path:
    """Create the directory (and any missing parents) if it doesn't exist yet."""
    resolved = Path(path).resolve()
    resolved.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    return resolved


def read_text(path: str | Path, encoding: str = "utf-8") -> str:
    """Read a text file and return its contents as a string."""
    return Path(path).read_text(encoding=encoding)


def write_text(path: str | Path, content: str, encoding: str = "utf-8") -> Path:
    """Write content to a file, creating parent directories if needed."""
    dest = Path(path).resolve()
    dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    dest.write_text(content, encoding=encoding)
    return dest


def read_bytes(path: str | Path) -> bytes:
    """Read a file and return its raw bytes."""
    return Path(path).read_bytes()


def write_bytes(path: str | Path, data: bytes) -> Path:
    """Write binary data to a file, creating parent directories if needed."""
    dest = Path(path).resolve()
    dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    dest.write_bytes(data)
    return dest


def safe_delete(path: str | Path) -> bool:
    """
    Delete a file or entire directory tree without raising if it doesn't exist.

    Returns True if something was deleted, False if the path was already gone.
    """
    target = Path(path)
    if not target.exists():
        return False
    if target.is_dir():
        shutil.rmtree(target)
    else:
        target.unlink()
    return True


def list_files(
    directory: str | Path,
    pattern: str = "*",
    recursive: bool = False,
) -> list[Path]:
    """
    Return a sorted list of files matching pattern inside directory.

    Set recursive=True to search all subdirectories as well.
    """
    root = Path(directory)
    globber = root.rglob if recursive else root.glob
    return sorted(p for p in globber(pattern) if p.is_file())


def file_size(path: str | Path) -> int:
    """Return the byte size of a file, or 0 if the file does not exist."""
    target = Path(path)
    return target.stat().st_size if target.exists() else 0


def resolve_project_path(*parts: str) -> Path:
    """
    Build an absolute path relative to the project root.

    The project root is one level above utils/ (workspace root).
    Example: resolve_project_path("data", "comp_db", "COMP_REPORTS.db")
    """
    project_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    return project_root.joinpath(*parts)


_JSON_OBJECT_PATTERN: re.Pattern[str] = re.compile(r"(\{.*?\}|\[.*?\])", re.DOTALL)


def safe_loads(text: str) -> Any:
    """Parse text as JSON; return None instead of raising on bad input."""
    try:
        return json.loads(text)
    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
        return None


def safe_dumps(obj: Any, indent: int = 2, default: Any = str) -> str:
    """Serialise obj to JSON, converting un-serialisable values with str()."""
    return json.dumps(obj, indent=indent, default=default, ensure_ascii=False)


def extract_json_from_text(text: str) -> Any:
    """
    Scan free-form text for the first valid JSON object or array and return it.

    Useful when the LLM wraps its JSON answer in explanatory prose.
    Returns None if no valid JSON block is found.
    """
    for match in _JSON_OBJECT_PATTERN.finditer(text):
        parsed = safe_loads(match.group(0))
        if parsed is not None:
            return parsed
    return None


def extract_json_array(text: str) -> list[Any]:
    """Return the first JSON array found in text, or [] if none exists."""
    result = extract_json_from_text(text)
    if isinstance(result, list):
        return result
    return []


def extract_json_object(text: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Return the first JSON object found in text, or {} if none exists."""
    result = extract_json_from_text(text)
    if isinstance(result, dict):
        return result
    return {}


def pretty_print(obj: Any) -> str:
    """Return a nicely indented JSON string for obj."""
    return safe_dumps(obj, indent=2)


def is_valid_json(text: str) -> bool:
    """Return True if text is syntactically valid JSON, False otherwise."""
    return safe_loads(text) is not None



####################################################################################################
FILE: utils\logger.py
####################################################################################################



"""
Centralised logging factory for the Compliance Chatbot.

All modules must obtain their logger exclusively through :func:`get_logger`
to guarantee consistent formatting and handler configuration.

Configuration is loaded from ``genai/config/logging.toml`` (TOML-based,
Python 3.11+ tomllib).  Falls back to a minimal ``basicConfig`` when the
TOML file is absent or unreadable.
"""

import logging
import logging.config
import logging.handlers
from pathlib import Path

_LOGGING_CONFIGURED: bool = False

# Project root is two levels up from utils/logger.py  → <root>/utils/logger.py
_PROJECT_ROOT: Path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
_TOML_CONFIG_PATH: Path = _PROJECT_ROOT / "genai" / "config" / "logging.toml"


def _load_toml_logging_config(path: Path) -> dict | None:
    """Load logging configuration from a TOML file. Returns None on failure."""
    try:
        try:
            import tomllib  # Python 3.11+
        except ImportError:
            try:
                import tomli as tomllib  # type: ignore[no-redef]
            except ImportError:
                return None

        with path.open("rb") as fh:
            data = tomllib.load(fh)
        return data.get("logging")
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
        return None


def _configure_logging(log_dir: str | None = None) -> None:
    """
    Bootstrap logging from ``genai/config/logging.toml``.

    Falls back to a minimal ``basicConfig`` if the TOML file is absent or
    the tomllib/tomli package is not available.

    Args:
        log_dir: Optional override for the log output directory.
    """
    global _LOGGING_CONFIGURED  # pylint: disable=global-statement

    if _LOGGING_CONFIGURED:
        return

    # Resolve log directory against project root so it is always
    # <project_root>/data/logs regardless of the process CWD.
    raw_dir = Path(log_dir) if log_dir else Path("data/logs")
    resolved_log_dir = raw_dir if raw_dir.is_absolute() else _PROJECT_ROOT / raw_dir
    resolved_log_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    log_config = _load_toml_logging_config(_TOML_CONFIG_PATH)

    if log_config:
        # Patch the rotating_file handler filename if a custom log_dir was given.
        handlers = log_config.get("handlers", {})
        rotating = handlers.get("rotating_file")
        if rotating:
            rotating["filename"] = str(resolved_log_dir / "compliance_bot.log")

        try:
            logging.config.dictConfig(log_config)
            _LOGGING_CONFIGURED = True
            return
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
            logging.getLogger(__name__).debug(
                "dictConfig failed — falling back to basicConfig."
            )

    # Fallback: simple basicConfig.
    logging.basicConfig(
        level=logging.INFO,
        format="%(asctime)s | %(levelname)-8s | %(name)s | %(message)s",
    )
    logging.getLogger(__name__).warning(
        "Logging TOML config not found or unreadable at %s — using basicConfig fallback.",
        _TOML_CONFIG_PATH,
    )

    _LOGGING_CONFIGURED = True


def get_logger(name: str, log_dir: str | None = None) -> logging.Logger:
    """
    Return a named logger, ensuring logging is configured beforehand.

    Args:
        name:    Dot-separated logger name (e.g. ``"compliance_bot.core"``).
        log_dir: Optional log output directory; used only on first call.

    Returns:
        Configured :class:`logging.Logger` instance.
    """
    _configure_logging(log_dir=log_dir)
    return logging.getLogger(name)



####################################################################################################
FILE: utils\registry.py
####################################################################################################



"""
utils/registry.py — data-access registries: databases, prompts, SQLite helpers.

Merges:
  - db_registry.py   — databases.toml metadata registry (tables, columns, few-shots)
  - db_utils.py      — read-only SQLite connection + query helpers
  - prompt_loader.py — YAML prompt file loader with in-memory cache

All three share the same concern: reading persistent data stores (TOML files
and SQLite databases) and caching the results in memory.
"""

import contextlib
import sqlite3
from collections.abc import Generator
from contextlib import contextmanager
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import yaml

from utils.logger import get_logger

LOGGER = get_logger("compliance_bot.utils.registry")


def _load_toml_file(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Open and parse a TOML file; return {} on any failure."""
    try:
        try:
            import tomllib  # built-in Python 3.11+
        except ImportError:
            import tomli as tomllib  # type: ignore[no-redef]
        with path.open("rb") as fh:
            return tomllib.load(fh)
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        LOGGER.warning("registry._load_toml_file — failed to load %s: %s", path, exc)
        return {}


_DATABASES_TOML = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "genai" / "config" / "databases.toml"

_db_registry: dict[str, Any] | None = None


def _build_db_registry() -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Parse databases.toml and return a flat {table_name: metadata} dict."""
    if not _DATABASES_TOML.exists():
        LOGGER.warning("registry: databases.toml not found at %s", _DATABASES_TOML)
        return {}

    raw = _load_toml_file(_DATABASES_TOML)
    registry: dict[str, Any] = {}

    for db_stem, db_data in raw.get("databases", {}).items():
        db_file = db_data.get("db_file", "")
        for table_name, table_data in db_data.get("tables", {}).items():
            few_shots_raw = table_data.get("few_shots", {})
            few_shots_str = (
                few_shots_raw.get("examples", "").strip()
                if isinstance(few_shots_raw, dict)
                else ""
            )
            registry[table_name] = {
                "db_stem":      db_stem,
                "db_file":      db_file,
                "description":  table_data.get("description", ""),
                "filter_query": table_data.get("filter_query", ""),
                "columns":      table_data.get("columns", {}),
                "few_shots":    few_shots_str,
            }
            LOGGER.debug("registry: registered table %s (db=%s)", table_name, db_stem)

    LOGGER.info("registry: loaded %d table(s) from databases.toml", len(registry))
    return registry


def _get_db_registry() -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Return the cached registry, building it on first call."""
    global _db_registry
    if _db_registry is None:
        _db_registry = _build_db_registry()
    return _db_registry


def reload() -> None:
    """Clear the database registry cache so the next access re-reads databases.toml."""
    global _db_registry
    _db_registry = None
    LOGGER.info("registry: db cache cleared — will reload on next access.")


def get_table_meta(table_name: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Return the full metadata dict for a registered table, or {} if unknown."""
    return _get_db_registry().get(table_name, {})


def get_few_shots(table_name: str) -> str:
    """Return the few-shot example block for a table, or '' if not registered."""
    return _get_db_registry().get(table_name, {}).get("few_shots", "")


def get_all_tables() -> list[str]:
    """Return a sorted list of every registered table name."""
    return sorted(_get_db_registry().keys())


def list_databases() -> list[str]:
    """Return a sorted list of every registered database stem name."""
    reg = _get_db_registry()
    return sorted({meta["db_stem"] for meta in reg.values()})


@contextmanager
def open_readonly(db_path: str | Path) -> Generator[sqlite3.Connection, None, None]:
    """
    Context manager: open a SQLite file in deferred read-only mode.

    Always rolls back and closes the connection on exit — even on error.
    Usage:
        with open_readonly("data/comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db") as conn:
            rows = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM COMP_TBL").fetchone()
    """
    path = Path(db_path)
    conn: sqlite3.Connection | None = None
    try:
        conn = sqlite3.connect(str(path), check_same_thread=False)
        conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        conn.execute("BEGIN DEFERRED")
        yield conn
    finally:
        if conn is not None:
            with contextlib.suppress(sqlite3.Error):
                conn.rollback()
            conn.close()


def fetch_all(
    db_path: str | Path,
    sql: str,
    params: tuple[Any, ...] = (),
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Run sql and return every result row as a list of {column: value} dicts."""
    with open_readonly(db_path) as conn:
        cursor = conn.execute(sql, params)
        col_names: list[str] = [d[0] for d in (cursor.description or [])]
        return [dict(zip(col_names, row, strict=False)) for row in cursor.fetchall()]


def fetch_scalar(
    db_path: str | Path,
    sql: str,
    params: tuple[Any, ...] = (),
    default: Any = None,
) -> Any:
    """Run sql and return just the first column of the first row (e.g. COUNT(*))."""
    with open_readonly(db_path) as conn:
        row = conn.execute(sql, params).fetchone()
    return row[0] if row else default


def list_tables(db_path: str | Path) -> list[str]:
    """Return a sorted list of all table names in the database."""
    sql = "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' ORDER BY name"
    with open_readonly(db_path) as conn:
        rows = conn.execute(sql).fetchall()
    return [row[0] for row in rows]


def table_exists(db_path: str | Path, table_name: str) -> bool:
    """Return True if table_name exists in the database (case-insensitive check)."""
    sql = "SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND UPPER(name)=UPPER(?)"
    return fetch_scalar(db_path, sql, (table_name,)) is not None


def get_column_names(db_path: str | Path, table_name: str) -> list[str]:
    """Return the ordered column names for a table via PRAGMA table_info."""
    with open_readonly(db_path) as conn:
        rows = conn.execute(f"PRAGMA table_info('{table_name}')").fetchall()
    return [row[1] for row in rows]


def row_count(db_path: str | Path, table_name: str) -> int:
    """Return how many rows are in the table; returns 0 on any error."""
    try:
        return fetch_scalar(db_path, f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM '{table_name}'", default=0)  # noqa: S608
    except sqlite3.Error as err:
        LOGGER.warning("registry.row_count — error on %s.%s: %s", db_path, table_name, err)
        return 0


_PROMPTS_DIR: Path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "genai" / "prompt"

_prompt_cache: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}


def _load_yaml_file(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Open and parse a YAML file; return {} on any failure."""
    try:
        with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            return yaml.safe_load(fh) or {}
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        LOGGER.warning("registry._load_yaml_file — failed to load %s: %s", path, exc)
        return {}


def load_prompt(filename: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Load and cache a YAML prompt file from genai/prompt/.

    filename — e.g. "sql_writer.yaml" (or bare stem "sql_writer")
    Returns the full parsed dict, or {} on error.
    Raises FileNotFoundError if the file does not exist.
    """
    stem = filename.removesuffix(".yaml").removesuffix(".toml")

    if stem in _prompt_cache:
        return _prompt_cache[stem]

    path = _PROMPTS_DIR / (stem + ".yaml")
    if not path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Prompt file not found: {path}")

    data = _load_yaml_file(path)
    _prompt_cache[stem] = data
    LOGGER.debug("registry: loaded prompt file: %s", path)
    return data


def get_prompt(filename: str, *keys: str, default: str | None = None) -> str | None:
    """
    Retrieve a nested string value from a YAML prompt file.

    Example: get_prompt("intent_checker.yaml", "messages", "clarification")

    Walks the key path and returns the string at that location,
    or default if any key is missing.
    """
    data = load_prompt(filename)
    node: Any = data
    for key in keys:
        if not isinstance(node, dict):
            return default
        node = node.get(key)
        if node is None:
            return default
    return str(node) if node is not None else default
